   921	    13	const STALE_AFTER_MS = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
   922	    14	
   923	    15	/** @typedef {{platform: string, origin: string, reason: string, gitSnapshot: any, probes?: any, sessionHint: string, sessionUnstable?: boolean, reasonClass?: string}} ReceiveOpts */
   924	    16	
   925	    17	/**
   926	    18	 * Classify the switch reason, KEEPING confidence (gate-2 iter-2 M4): a
   927	    19	 * low-confidence heuristic (e.g. Cursor's "quota exceeded") must not silently
   928	    20	 * drive an avoid[] decision — the caller warns and defers to explicit intake.
   929	    21	 * @param {string} reason @param {string} originPlatform @param {any} io
   930	    22	 * @returns {{class: string | null, confidence: string | null}}
   931	    23	 */
   932	    24	function classifyReason(reason, originPlatform, io) {
   933	    25	  try {
   934	    26	    const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
   935	    27	    const verdict = classify({ text: reason, exitCode: 0, platform: originPlatform, table, structured: null });
   936	    28	    return verdict.class === 'ok' ? { class: null, confidence: null } : { class: verdict.class, confidence: verdict.confidence };
   937	    29	  } catch {
   938	    30	    return { class: null, confidence: null };
   939	    31	  }
   940	    32	}
   941	    33	
   942	    34	/**
   943	    35	 * Effective intake: the sealed bundle already records who handed off and why,
   944	    36	 * so the 'unknown'/'unspecified' CLI sentinels default from it (audit finding:
   945	    37	 * the documented resume command rendered "Handed off from unknown" straight
   946	    38	 * after sealing a real origin/reason). Explicit intake always wins. Pure and
   947	    39	 * bundle-deterministic, so prepare and commit derive identical tokens.
   948	    40	 * @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @returns {ReceiveOpts}
   949	    41	 */
   950	    42	function effectiveIntake(bundle, opts) {
   951	    43	  const originSentinel = opts.origin === 'unknown' || opts.origin === undefined;
   952	    44	  const reasonSentinel = opts.reason === 'unspecified' || opts.reason === undefined;
   953	    45	  const sealedOrigin = typeof bundle?.origin?.platform === 'string' && bundle.origin.platform !== 'unknown' ? bundle.origin.platform : null;
   954	    46	  const sealedReason = typeof bundle?.handoff?.reason === 'string' && bundle.handoff.reason.length > 0 ? bundle.handoff.reason : null;
   955	    47	  return {
   956	    48	    ...opts,
   957	    49	    origin: originSentinel && sealedOrigin ? sealedOrigin : opts.origin,
   958	    50	    reason: reasonSentinel && sealedReason ? sealedReason : opts.reason,
   959	    51	  };
   960	    52	}
   961	    53	
   962	    54	// The bound token inputs, in the fixed order the structured token serializes
   963	    55	// them. The human names power drift diagnosis: a stale-token rejection names
   964	    56	// exactly which input(s) moved instead of listing six guesses.
   965	    57	const TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
   966	    58	  ['generation', 'bundle generation'],
   967	    59	  ['journalSeq', 'journal seq'],
   968	    60	  ['handoffStatus', 'handoff status'],
   969	    61	  ['receives', 'receive count'],
   970	    62	  ['origin', 'intake origin'],
   971	    63	  ['reason', 'intake reason'],
   972	    64	  ['reasonClass', 'intake reason-class'],
   973	    65	  ['configDigest', 'config'],
   974	    66	  ['gitDigest', 'git state'],
   975	    67	  ['probesDigest', 'probe cache'],
   976	    68	  ['platform', 'target platform'],
   977	    69	  ['sessionHint', 'target session'],
   978	    70	]);
   979	    71	const TOKEN_PREFIX = 'rcpt1';
   980	    72	
   981	    73	/**
   982	    74	 * The revision fingerprint the receipt token derives from. Every field is a
   983	    75	 * bound input: any drift between prepare and commit re-derives a different
   984	    76	 * token, so a stale receipt can never commit (plan §Concurrency).
   985	    77	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
   986	    78	 * @returns {Record<string, any>}
   987	    79	 */
   988	    80	function tokenInputs(root, bundle, opts, io) {
   989	    81	  let configDigest = 'absent';
   990	    82	  try {
   991	    83	    configDigest = dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(`${root}/baton.config.json`, 'utf8'));
   992	    84	  } catch {
   993	    85	    // no config — 'absent' is itself a bound value
   994	    86	  }
   995	    87	  return {
   996	    88	    generation: bundle.generation,
   997	    89	    journalSeq: bundle.journalSeq,
   998	    90	    // The seal state is a bound input too (gate-2 fix): a competing receiver
   999	    91	    // that lands between this token's derivation and its commit changes
  1000	    92	    // status/receive_log, so the loser's token cannot re-derive.
  1001	    93	    handoffStatus: bundle.handoff?.status ?? null,
  1002	    94	    receives: Array.isArray(bundle.handoff?.receive_log) ? bundle.handoff.receive_log.length : 0,
  1003	    95	    origin: opts.origin,
  1004	    96	    reason: opts.reason,
  1005	    97	    // Explicit --reason-class is a bound intake input (gate-2 iter-2 M4): it
  1006	    98	    // changes the avoid[] decision, so a change between prepare and commit is
  1007	    99	    // drift that must re-prepare.
  1008	   100	    reasonClass: opts.reasonClass ?? null,
  1009	   101	    configDigest,
  1010	   102	    gitDigest: dedupeKey(opts.gitSnapshot ?? null),
  1011	   103	    probesDigest: dedupeKey(opts.probes ?? null),
  1012	   104	    platform: opts.platform,
  1013	   105	    sessionHint: opts.sessionHint,
  1014	   106	  };
  1015	   107	}
  1016	   108	
  1017	   109	/**
  1018	   110	 * Structured receipt token: a fixed-order join of per-input digests. Equality
  1019	   111	 * still gates commit exactly as the old opaque hash did; the structure only
  1020	   112	 * buys DIAGNOSIS — a mismatch can name the drifted input(s).
  1021	   113	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
  1022	   114	 */
  1023	   115	function deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io) {
  1024	   116	  const inputs = tokenInputs(root, bundle, opts, io);
  1025	   117	  return `${TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + TOKEN_FIELDS.map(([key]) => dedupeKey(inputs[key] ?? null).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
  1026	   118	}
  1027	   119	
  1028	   120	/**
  1029	   121	 * Name the inputs whose digests differ between two structured tokens.
  1030	   122	 * @param {string} presented @param {string} expected @returns {string[]}
  1031	   123	 */
  1032	   124	function driftedInputs(presented, expected) {
  1033	   125	  const a = presented.split('.');
  1034	   126	  const b = expected.split('.');
  1035	   127	  if (a[0] !== TOKEN_PREFIX || b[0] !== TOKEN_PREFIX || a.length !== b.length) return [];
  1036	   128	  return TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((_, i) => a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]).map(([, name]) => name);
  1037	   129	}
  1038	   130	
  1039	   131	/**
  1040	   132	 * @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
  1041	   133	 * @returns {string[]} staleness/degradation warnings
  1042	   134	 */
  1043	   135	function stalenessWarnings(bundle, opts, io) {
  1044	   136	  /** @type {string[]} */
  1045	   137	  const warnings = [];
  1046	   138	  if (bundle.handoff.status === 'open') {
  1047	   139	    warnings.push('source bundle is unsealed (open) — the handoff was never finalized; treating this as a degraded (limit-death) receive');
  1048	   140	  }
  1049	   141	  const ref = bundle.handoff.finalizedAt ?? bundle.updatedAt;
  1050	   142	  const ageMs = Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(ref);
  1051	   143	  if (Number.isFinite(ageMs) && ageMs > STALE_AFTER_MS) {
  1052	   144	    warnings.push(`bundle finalized ${Math.round(ageMs / 3_600_000)}h ago (staleness threshold 12h) — re-verify before continuing`);
  1053	   145	  }
  1054	   146	  const g = opts.gitSnapshot;
  1055	   147	  if (g && bundle.git) {
  1056	   148	    if (bundle.git.headSha !== g.headSha) {
  1057	   149	      warnings.push(`HEAD moved since capture: bundle recorded ${bundle.git.headSha}, working tree now ${g.headSha}`);
  1058	   150	    }
  1059	   151	    if (bundle.git.dirty !== g.dirty) {
  1060	   152	      warnings.push(`dirty-state mismatch: bundle captured dirty=${bundle.git.dirty}, working tree now dirty=${g.dirty}`);
  1061	   153	    }
  1062	   154	  }
  1063	   155	  return warnings;
  1064	   156	}
  1065	   157	
  1066	   158	/**
  1067	   159	 * Phase 1 of the receive transaction: validate, resolve roles, render the
  1068	   160	 * resume prompt, and return a one-time receipt token. Mutates NOTHING — the
  1069	   161	 * token is self-contained (a fingerprint hash), never a persisted record.
  1070	   162	 * @param {string} root @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
  1071	   163	 * @returns {{token: string, prompt: string, assignments: Record<string, any>, warnings: string[]}}
  1072	   164	 */
  1073	   165	export function prepare(root, opts, io) {
  1074	   166	  const { bundle, warnings: loadWarnings } = loadBundle(root, io);
  1075	   167	  if (bundle === null) throw new Error(`no handoff bundle under ${root}/.handoff — nothing to receive`);
  1076	   168	  opts = effectiveIntake(bundle, opts);
  1077	   169	
  1078	   170	  const warnings = [...loadWarnings, ...stalenessWarnings(bundle, opts, io)];
  1079	   171	
  1080	   172	  const { config, errors } = loadConfig(root, io);
  1081	   173	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
  1082	   174	  let assignments = {};
  1083	   175	  if (config) {
  1084	   176	    // The dead origin comes from the BUNDLE first (its sealed reasonClass and
  1085	   177	    // recorded origin platform); typed intake only fills the gaps — a user
  1086	   178	    // should not need to retype the verbatim limit string to keep roles off
  1087	   179	    // the platform that died (gate-2 reviewer-b finding 4). Confidence gates
  1088	   180	    // auto-avoidance (gate-2 iter-2 M4): an explicit --reason-class or a sealed
  1089	   181	    // reasonClass is trusted; a live text classification only auto-avoids at
  1090	   182	    // medium+ confidence, else it warns and defers to explicit intake.
  1091	   183	    let reasonClass;
  1092	   184	    let confidence;
  1093	   185	    if (typeof opts.reasonClass === 'string') {
  1094	   186	      reasonClass = opts.reasonClass;
  1095	   187	      confidence = 'high'; // explicit user intake
  1096	   188	    } else if (typeof bundle.handoff?.reasonClass === 'string') {
  1097	   189	      reasonClass = bundle.handoff.reasonClass;
  1098	   190	      confidence = 'high'; // trusted seal
  1099	   191	    } else {
  1100	   192	      const v = classifyReason(opts.reason, opts.origin, io);
  1101	   193	      reasonClass = v.class;
  1102	   194	      confidence = v.confidence;
  1103	   195	    }
  1104	   196	    const deadOrigin =
  1105	   197	      typeof bundle.origin?.platform === 'string' && bundle.origin.platform !== 'unknown' ? bundle.origin.platform : opts.origin;
  1106	   198	    /** @type {string[]} */
  1107	   199	    let avoid = [];
  1108	   200	    if (reasonClass === 'usage-limit') {
  1109	   201	      if (confidence === 'low') {
  1110	   202	        warnings.push(
  1111	   203	          `the switch reason matched a usage-limit heuristic only at LOW confidence — not auto-avoiding ${deadOrigin}; pass --reason-class usage-limit to confirm the failover away from it`,
  1112	   204	        );
  1113	   205	      } else {
  1114	   206	        avoid = [deadOrigin];
  1115	   207	      }
  1116	   208	    }
  1117	   209	    assignments = resolveRoles({ config, to: opts.platform, avoid, nativeOnly: false, probes: opts.probes ?? null }).assignments;
  1118	   210	  } else {
  1119	   211	    warnings.push(`role matrix unavailable (${errors.map((e) => e.msg).join('; ')}) — no role table in this prompt`);
  1120	   212	  }
  1121	   213	
  1122	   214	  const prompt = renderResumePrompt({ bundle, assignments, warnings, origin: opts.origin, reason: opts.reason });
  1123	   215	  return { token: deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io), prompt, assignments, warnings };
  1124	   216	}
  1125	   217	
  1126	   218	/**
  1127	   219	 * Phase 2: one atomic transition. Re-derives every bound input from current
  1128	   220	 * disk state + opts and rejects ANY drift (throws, zero mutation). On match:
  1129	   221	 * writes the received seal, archives it via a 'receive' rotation, then opens a
  1130	   222	 * fresh writable generation owned by the receiving platform/session.
  1131	   223	 * @param {string} root @param {string} token @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
  1132	   224	 * @returns {{adopted: boolean, generation: number, archivedTo: string | null, alreadyCommitted?: boolean}}
  1133	   225	 */
  1134	   226	export function commit(root, token, opts, io) {
  1135	   227	  // ONE operation-scoped lock across revalidation and every mutation (gate-2
  1136	   228	  // fix: reviewers a#1/b#11): two competing receivers serialize here, and the
  1137	   229	  // loser's re-derivation sees the winner's generation/status/receive_log.
  1138	   230	  return withLock(root, io, (fence) => commitLocked(root, token, opts, io, fence));
  1139	   231	}
  1140	   232	
  1141	   233	/**
  1142	   234	 * @param {string} root @param {string} token @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io @param {string} fence
  1143	   235	 * @returns {{adopted: boolean, generation: number, archivedTo: string | null, alreadyCommitted?: boolean}}
  1144	   236	 */
  1145	   237	function commitLocked(root, token, opts, io, fence) {
  1146	   238	  const { bundle } = loadBundle(root, io);
  1147	   239	  if (bundle === null) throw new Error(`no handoff bundle under ${root}/.handoff — nothing to receive; run receive --prepare first`);
  1148	   240	  opts = effectiveIntake(bundle, opts);
  1149	   241	
  1150	   242	  const expected = deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io);
  1151	   243	  if (expected !== token) {
  1152	   244	    // Idempotency first (audit finding: duplicate-receive corruption): if THIS
  1153	   245	    // exact receipt already landed — its digest is recorded on a receive_log
  1154	   246	    // entry — the retry is a lost-output re-send, not drift. Report success
  1155	   247	    // with zero mutation instead of sending the model into a re-prepare +
  1156	   248	    // re-commit loop that executes a second full receive.
  1157	   249	    const receipt = dedupeKey(token).slice(0, 16);
  1158	   250	    const landed = Array.isArray(bundle.handoff?.receive_log) && bundle.handoff.receive_log.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e?.receipt === receipt);
  1159	   251	    if (landed) {
  1160	   252	      return { adopted: false, alreadyCommitted: true, generation: bundle.generation, archivedTo: null };
  1161	   253	    }
  1162	   254	    const drifted = driftedInputs(token, expected);
  1163	   255	    const detail = drifted.length > 0 ? `drifted input(s): ${drifted.join(', ')}` : 'a bound input drifted since prepare';
  1164	   256	    throw new Error(
  1165	   257	      `receipt token is stale — ${detail}. Re-prepare and retry, passing the SAME intake flags (--origin/--reason/--reason-class) to both prepare and --commit`,
  1166	   258	    );
  1167	   259	  }
  1168	   260	
  1169	   261	  const degraded = bundle.handoff.status !== 'sealed';
  1170	   262	  const receiveEntry = {
  1171	   263	    origin: opts.origin,
  1172	   264	    reason: opts.reason,
  1173	   265	    at: io.now(),
  1174	   266	    // The receipt digest keys the idempotent-retry detection above.
  1175	   267	    receipt: dedupeKey(token).slice(0, 16),
  1176	   268	    ...(degraded ? { degradedSeal: true } : {}),
  1177	   269	  };
  1178	   270	
  1179	   271	  // The degraded seal must NOT persist a LOW-confidence classification (iter-3
  1180	   272	  // F10): a sealed reasonClass is trusted as high-confidence by the next
  1181	   273	  // prepare, so laundering a low-confidence heuristic here would silently
  1182	   274	  // auto-avoid the origin one hop later. Explicit intake is high-confidence and
  1183	   275	  // seals; a live classification seals ONLY at medium+ confidence, else null
  1184	   276	  // (the next receive re-classifies the reason text at its true confidence).
  1185	   277	  let sealedReasonClass = null;
  1186	   278	  if (degraded) {
  1187	   279	    if (typeof opts.reasonClass === 'string') {
  1188	   280	      sealedReasonClass = opts.reasonClass;
  1189

  1252	     6	/**
  1253	     7	 * `baton receive` — the two-phase resume flow. `--print-prompt` and
  1254	     8	 * `--prepare` are read-only (prepare mutates nothing); `--commit <token>`
  1255	     9	 * performs the atomic transition. The receiving session hint defaults to a
  1256	    10	 * host-stable value so prepare and commit issued by separate CLI processes
  1257	    11	 * still derive the same token; adapters pass `--session` with their real id.
  1258	    12	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io

  1260	    14	 */
  1261	    15	export async function cmdReceive(args, io) {
  1262	    16	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', origin: 'string', reason: 'string', 'reason-class': 'string', session: 'string', commit: 'string', prepare: 'boolean', 'print-prompt': 'boolean' });

  1268	    22	  if (pErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'receive', pErr);
  1269	    23	
  1270	    24	  // Validate --reason-class against the class vocabulary BEFORE it is trusted as

  1436	     1	/**
  1437	     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
  1438	     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
  1439	     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
  1440	     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
  1441	     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
  1442	     7	 */
  1443	     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
  1444	     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
  1445	    10	  const notes = [];
  1446	    11	
  1447	    12	  /**
  1448	    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
  1449	    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
  1450	    15	   */
  1451	    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
  1452	    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
  1453	    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
  1454	    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
  1455	    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
  1456	    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
  1457	    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
  1458	    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
  1459	    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
  1460	    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
  1461	    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
  1462	    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
  1463	    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
  1464	    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
  1465	    30	    }
  1466	    31	    return null;
  1467	    32	  }
  1468	    33	
  1469	    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
  1470	    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
  1471	    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
  1472	    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
  1473	    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
  1474	    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
  1475	    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
  1476	    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
  1477	    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
  1478	    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
  1479	    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
  1480	    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
  1481	    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
  1482	    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
  1483	    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
  1484	    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
  1485	    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
  1486	    51	  };
  1487	    52	
  1488	    53	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
  1489	    54	  const assignments = {};
  1490	    55	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
  1491	    56	    /** @type {any[]} */
  1492	    57	    const skipped = [];
  1493	    58	    /** @type {any} */
  1494	    59	    let assignment = null;
  1495	    60	
  1496	    61	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
  1497	    62	      const entry = chain[i];
  1498	    63	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
  1499	    64	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
  1500	    65	        continue;
  1501	    66	      }
  1502	    67	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
  1503	    68	      if (why) {
  1504	    69	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
  1505	    70	        continue;
  1506	    71	      }
  1507	    72	      assignment = {
  1508	    73	        platform: entry.platform,
  1509	    74	        model: entry.model,
  1510	    75	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
  1511	    76	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
  1512	    77	        chainIndex: i,
  1513	    78	        skipped,
  1514	    79	      };
  1515	    80	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
  1516	    81	      break;
  1517	    82	    }
  1518	    83	
  1519	    84	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
  1520	    85	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
  1521	    86	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
  1522	    87	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
  1523	    88	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
  1524	    89	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
  1525	    90	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
  1526	    91	      }
  1527	    92	    }
  1528	    93	
  1529	    94	    if (!assignment) {
  1530	    95	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
  1531	    96	    }
  1532	    97	    assignments[role] = assignment;
  1533	    98	  }
  1534	    99	
  1535	   100	  return { assignments, notes };
  1536	   101	}

  1623	    15	 * which is not a switch reason — degrade to null rather than mislabel.

  1627	    19	function inferReasonClass(reason, platform, io) {

  1636	    28	
  1637	    29	/**
  1638	    30	 * `baton finalize` — the narrative seal: records the switch reason, refreshes
  1639	    31	 * git state, seals the bundle, and freezes this generation into history via a
  1640	    32	 * 'finalize' journal rotation.
  1641	    33	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io

  1643	    35	 */
  1644	    36	export async function cmdFinalize(args, io) {
  1645	    37	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { reason: 'string', 'reason-class': 'string', to: 'string' });
  1646	    38	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'finalize', parsed.error);
  1647	    39	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  1648	    40	  const reason = typeof flags.reason === 'string' ? flags.reason : null;

  1650	    42	  const toErr = platformError(flags.to);

  1652	    44	  const REASON_CLASSES = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
  1653	    45	  if (flags['reason-class'] !== undefined && !REASON_CLASSES.includes(/** @type {string} */ (flags['reason-class']))) {
  1654	    46	    return usageError(io, flags, 'finalize', `--reason-class must be one of ${REASON_CLASSES.join('|')}`);
  1655	    47	  }
  1656	    48	
  1657	    49	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);

  1661	    53	  if (bundle === null) {

  1663	    55	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error });

  1667	    59	
  1668	    60	  // Capture git BEFORE the lock — it is async and must not straddle the lock.

  1671	    63	
  1672	    64	  // ONE atomic locked span (iter-5 A2): the seal, snapshot write, and journal
  1673	    65	  // rotation run under a SINGLE lock acquisition + fence, and the bundle is
  1674	    66	  // RELOADED inside the lock. The old code loaded before the async git await

  1676	    68	  // during the await was sealed from a stale bundle and survived only in the
  1677	    69	  // rotated journal. Reloading inside the lock includes it in the seal.

  1719	   111	    );

  1724	   116	  } else {

  1727	   119	  }
  1728	   120	  return 0;

  1805	     2

  1807	     4	const STOP_FAILURE_MAP = {
  1808	     5	  rate_limit: 'usage-limit',
  1809	     6	  overloaded: 'throttle',
  1810	     7	  authentication_failed: 'auth',
  1811	     8	  oauth_org_not_allowed: 'auth',
  1812	     9	  billing_error: 'auth',
  1813	    10	};

  1816	    13	const ANSI = /\x1b\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z]/g;
  1817	    14	
  1818	    15	/** @param {any} matcher @param {string} text */
  1819	    16	function matches(matcher, text) {
  1820	    17	  switch (matcher.kind) {
  1821	    18	    case 'substring':
  1822	    19	      return text.includes(matcher.value);
  1823	    20	    case 'regex':
  1824	    21	      return new RegExp(matcher.pattern, matcher.flags || '').test(text);
  1825	    22	    case 'json-field': {
  1826	    23	      for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
  1827	    24	        let obj;
  1828	    25	        try {
  1829	    26	          obj = JSON.parse(line);
  1830	    27	        } catch {
  1831	    28	          continue;
  1832	    29	        }
  1833	    30	        const val = String(matcher.path)
  1834	    31	          .split('.')
  1835	    32	          .reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
  1836	    33	        if (val === matcher.equals) return true;
  1837	    34	      }
  1838	    35	      return false;
  1839	    36	    }
  1840	    37	    default:
  1841	    38	      return false;
  1842	    39	  }
  1843	    40	}

  1845	    42	/**
  1846	    43	 * Classify harness output. Structured evidence first (text and exit code are
  1847	    44	 * ignored when a recognized structured signal is present); text signatures as
  1848	    45	 * the fallback tier; exit code only breaks the no-match tie — a generic nonzero
  1849	    46	 * exit is never usage-limit on its own.
  1850	    47	 * @param {{text?: string, exitCode?: number, platform: string, table: any, structured?: {kind: string, errorType: string} | null}} input
  1851	    48	 * @returns {{class: string, signatureId: string | null, confidence: string | null, resetHint: string | null, tried?: string[]}}
  1852	    49	 */
  1853	    50	export function classify({ text = '', exitCode = 0, platform, table, structured = null }) {
  1854	    51	  if (structured) {
  1855	    52	    if (structured.kind === 'stop-failure') {
  1856	    53	      const cls = STOP_FAILURE_MAP[structured.errorType] ?? 'other-error';
  1857	    54	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
  1858	    55	    }
  1859	    56	    if (structured.kind === 'exec-json') {
  1860	    57	      const cls = structured.errorType === 'usage_limit' ? 'usage-limit' : 'other-error';
  1861	    58	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
  1862	    59	    }
  1863	    60	    // Unrecognized structured kind: fall through to the text tier.
  1864	    61	  }
  1865	    62	
  1866	    63	  const stripped = text.replace(ANSI, '');
  1867	    64	  const candidates = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform);
  1868	    65	  /** @type {any} */
  1869	    66	  let best = null;
  1870	    67	  for (const s of candidates) {
  1871	    68	    if (!matches(s.matcher, stripped)) continue;
  1872	    69	    if (best === null || PRECEDENCE.indexOf(s.class) < PRECEDENCE.indexOf(best.class)) best = s;
  1873	    70	  }
  1874	    71	
  1875	    72	  if (best) {
  1876	    73	    let resetHint = null;
  1877	    74	    if (typeof best.resetHint === 'string') {
  1878	    75	      const m = stripped.match(new RegExp(best.resetHint));
  1879	    76	      if (m) resetHint = m[1] ?? m[0];
  1880	    77	    }
  1881	    78	    return { class: best.class, signatureId: best.id, confidence: best.confidence, resetHint };
  1882	    79	  }
  1883	    80	
  1884	    81	  return {
  1885	    82	    class: exitCode !== 0 ? 'other-error' : 'ok',
  1886	    83	    signatureId: null,
  1887	    84	    confidence: null,
  1888	    85	    resetHint: null,
  1889	    86	  };
  1890	    87	}

  1908	    12	/** @param {string} root */
  1909	    13	export function bundlePaths(root) {
  1910	    14	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
  1911	    15	  return {
  1912	    16	    dir,
  1913	    17	    snapshot: `${dir}/bundle.json`,
  1914	    18	    bak: `${dir}/bundle.json.bak`,
  1915	    19	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
  1916	    20	    handoffMd: `${dir}/HANDOFF.md`,
  1917	    21	    historyDir: `${dir}/history`,

  1920	    24	  };
  1921	    25	}
  1922	    26	
  1923	    27	/** @param {string} iso */
  1924	    28	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
  1925	    29	
  1926	    30	/** @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p */
  1927	    31	const markerPath = (p) => `${p.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;
  1928	    32	
  1929	    33	/**
  1930	    34	 * Reconcile a leftover rotation marker (crash recovery, part of load). The
  1931	    35	 * mutations run under the repo lock, acquired WITHOUT waiting (gate-2 minor
  1932	    36	 * 16): a held lock means someone is mid-operation — reconciliation defers to
  1933	    37	 * the next unlocked load instead of writing behind their back.
  1934	    38	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings @param {string} root
  1935	    39	 */
  1936	    40	function reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root) {
  1937	    41	  const mp = markerPath(p);
  1938	    42	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return;
  1939	    43	  try {
  1940	    44	    withLock(root, { ...io, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } }, () => {
  1941	    45	      if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return; // a competitor reconciled while we acquired
  1942	    46	      const marker = safeReadJson(io.fs, mp);
  1943	    47	      if (!marker.ok) {
  1944	    48	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  1945	    49	        warnings.push('rotation marker was unparseable — rolled the rotation back (marker cleared, journal preserved)');
  1946	    50	        return;
  1947	    51	      }
  1948	    52	      const stem = `${tsForFile(marker.value.startedAt)}.${marker.value.kind}`;
  1949	    53	      const freeze = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`;
  1950	    54	      const rotated = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`;
  1951	    55	
  1952	    56	      if (io.fs.existsSync(freeze)) {
  1953	    57	        // Roll forward: finish whichever step the crash interrupted.
  1954	    58	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal) && !io.fs.existsSync(rotated)) {
  1955	    59	          io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, rotated);
  1956	    60	        }
  1957	    61	        if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1958	    62	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
  1959	    63	        }
  1960	    64	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  1961	    65	        warnings.push(`completed an interrupted rotation (roll-forward of ${stem}); marker cleared`);
  1962	    66	      } else {
  1963	    67	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  1964	    68	        warnings.push(`aborted an incomplete rotation (roll-back; ${stem} freeze never landed); marker cleared, journal preserved`);
  1965	    69	      }
  1966	    70	    });
  1967	    71	  } catch (err) {
  1968	    72	    if (err instanceof LockHeldError) {
  1969	    73	      warnings.push('a rotation marker is present but the repo lock is held — reconciliation deferred to the next unlocked load');
  1970	    74	      return;
  1971	    75	    }
  1972	    76	    throw err;
  1973	    77	  }
  1974	    78	}
  1975	    79	
  1976	    80	/**
  1977	    81	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings
  1978	    82	 * @returns {any | null} a usable base bundle, or null when nothing exists
  1979	    83	 */
  1980	    84	function resolveBase(io, p, warnings) {
  1981	    85	  const snapshotExists = io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot);
  1982	    86	  if (snapshotExists) {
  1983	    87	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  1984	    88	    if (snap.ok && validateBundle(snap.value).ok) return snap.value;
  1985	    89	    warnings.push(
  1986	    90	      snap.ok
  1987	    91	        ? 'snapshot bundle.json failed schema validation — attempting recovery'
  1988	    92	        : `snapshot bundle.json is unreadable (${snap.error}) — attempting recovery`,
  1989	    93	    );
  1990	    94	    const bak = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.bak);
  1991	    95	    if (bak.ok && validateBundle(bak.value).ok) {
  1992	    96	      warnings.push('recovered from backup bundle.json.bak; replaying journal past its journalSeq');
  1993	    97	      return bak.value;
  1994	    98	    }
  1995	    99	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.bak)) warnings.push('backup bundle.json.bak is also unusable');
  1996	   100	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1997	   101	      warnings.push('rebuilding the bundle by replaying the whole journal over a fresh seed (recovery of last resort)');
  1998	   102	      return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
  1999	   103	    }
  2000	   104	    return null;
  2001	   105	  }
  2002	   106	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  2003	   107	    warnings.push('no snapshot present — rebuilding the bundle from the journal');
  2004	   108	    return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
  2005	   109	  }
  2006	   110	  return null;
  2007	   111	}
  2008	   112	
  2009	   113	/**
  2010	   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
  2011	   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
  2012	   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
  2013	   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
  2014	   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
  2015	   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  2016	   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
  2017	   121	 */
  2018	   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
  2019	   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  2020	   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2021	   125	  const warnings = [];
  2022	   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
  2023	   127	
  2024	   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
  2025	   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
  2026	   130	
  2027	   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
  2028	   132	
  2029	   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
  2030	   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
  2031	   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
  2032	   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
  2033	   137	  }
  2034	   138	
  2035	   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
  2036	   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
  2037	   141	
  2038	   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  2039	   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
  2040	   144	    for (const w of jw) {
  2041	   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
  2042	   146	    }
  2043	   147	    for (const e of entries) {
  2044	   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
  2045	   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
  2046	   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.
  2047	   151	      if (e === null || typeof e !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e)) {
  2048	   152	        warnings.push('skipped a malformed journal line (not an event envelope)');
  2049	   153	        continue;
  2050	   154	      }
  2051	   155	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq <= bundle.journalSeq) continue;
  2052	   156	      bundle = applyEvent(bundle, e);
  2053	   157	    }
  2054	   158	  }
  2055	   159	  return { bundle, warnings };
  2056	   160	}
  2057	   161	
  2058	   162	/**
  2059	   163	 * Lock-context snapshot write: caller already holds the repo lock and passes
  2060	   164	 * its fencing token; the write is fence-checked immediately before landing
  2061	   165	 * (the fast-abort layer — gate-2 fix, reviewer-b finding 12).
  2062	   166	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io @param {string} token
  2063	   167	 */
  2064	   168	export function writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token) {
  2065	   169	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  2066	   170	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  2067	   171	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  2068	   172	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  2069	   173	    // Size discipline runs in the write path itself (gate-2 fix 10): every
  2070	   174	    // persisted snapshot is within budget; identity for in-budget bundles.
  2071	   175	    const bounded = compact(bundle);
  2072	   176	    // Validate-before-backup (gate-2 major 15): a corrupt current snapshot
  2073	   177	    // must never overwrite a good .bak — the backup only rotates when the
  2074	   178	    // outgoing snapshot itself would be recoverable.
  2075	   179	    const prev = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  2076	   180	    if (prev.ok && validateBundle(prev.value).ok) {
  2077	   181	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.bak, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
  2078	   182	    }
  2079	   183	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.snapshot, JSON.stringify(bounded, null, 2) + '\n');
  2080	   184	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.handoffMd, renderHandoffMd(bounded));
  2081	   185	  });
  2082	   186	}
  2083	   187	
  2084	   188	/**
  2085	   189	 * Persist a snapshot: back up the previous good one, write atomically, render
  2086	   190	 * HANDOFF.md alongside. Mutation — runs under the repo lock.
  2087	   191	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io
  2088	   192	 */
  2089	   193	export function writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) {
  2090	   194	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token));
  2091	   195	}
  2092	   196	
  2093	   197	/**
  2094	   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
  2095	   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
  2096	   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
  2097	   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
  2098	   202	 */
  2099	   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
  2100	   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  2101	   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
  2102	   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  2103	   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  2104	   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  2105	   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
  2106	   210	      let toAppend = entry;
  2107	   211	      if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
  2108	   212	        const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  2109	   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  2110	   214	        let tailSeq = 0;
  2111	   215	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  2112	   216	          for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
  2113	   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
  2114	   218	          }
  2115	   219	        }
  2116	   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
  2117	   221	        toAppend = { ...entry, seq };
  2118	   222	      }
  2119	   223	      appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, toAppend);
  2120	   224	      return seq;
  2121	   225	    }),
  2122	   226	  );
  2123	   227	}
  2124	   228	
  2125	   229	/**
  2126	   230	 * Crash-safe journal rotation (marker → freeze → rename → fresh journal →
  2127	   231	 * clear marker), with 10-per-kind history retention. Mutation — lock-gated.
  2128	   232	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io
  2129	   233	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
  2130	   234	 */
  2131	   235	export function rotateJournal(root, kind, io) {
  2132	   236	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token));
  2133	   237	}
  2134	   238	
  2135	   239	/**
  2136	   240	 * Lock-context rotation: caller holds the lock and passes its fencing token.
  2137	   241	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io @param {string} token
  2138	   242	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
  2139	   243	 */
  2140	   244	export function rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token) {
  2141	   245	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  2142	   246	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  2143	   247	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  2144	   248	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  2145	   249	    const startedAt = io.now();
  2146	   250	    const stem = `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`;
  2147	   251	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  2148	   252	    const seq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  2149	   253	
  2150	   254	    atomicWriteJson(io.fs, markerPath(p), { kind, startedAt, seq });
  2151	   255	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.historyDir);
  2152	   256	
  2153	   257	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot)) {
  2154	   258	      // Atomic freeze (gate-2 minor 16): tmp+rename, so a crash mid-copy can
  2155	   259	      // never leave a partial history snapshot that recovery would trust.
  2156	   260	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
  2157	   261	    } else {
  2158	   262	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, '');
  2159	   263	    }
  2160	   264	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  2161	   265	      io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`);
  2162	   266	    } else {
  2163	   267	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`, '');
  2164	   268	    }
  2165	   269	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
  2166	   270	
  2167	   271	    // Retention: newest RETAIN_PER_KIND per kind (ISO-derived stems sort by time).
  2168	   272	    const freezes = io.fs
  2169	   273	      .readdirSync(p.historyDir)
  2170	   274	      .filter((/** @type {string} */ n) => n.endsWith(`.${kind}.json`))
  2171	   275	      .sort();
  2172	   276	    while (freezes.length > RETAIN_PER_KIND) {
  2173	   277	      const oldest = /** @type {string} */ (freezes.shift());
  2174	   278	      const oldStem = oldest.slice(0, -'.json'.length);
  2175	   279	      for (const suffix of ['.json', '.ndjson']) {
  2176	   280	        try {
  2177	   281	          io.fs.unlinkSync(`${p.historyDir}/${oldStem}${suffix}`);
  2178	   282	        } catch {
  2179	   283	          // already absent — retention is best-effort per file
  2180	   284	        }
  2181	   285	      }
  2182	   286	    }
  2183	   287	
  2184	   288	    io.fs.unlinkSync(markerPath(p));
  2185	   289	    return stem;
  2186	   290	  });
  2187	   291	}
  2188	
  2189	 succeeded in 0ms:
  2190	     1	import { atomicWriteJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
  2191	     2	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
  2192	     3	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
  2193	     4	import { checkHandoffTree } from '../util/jail.mjs';
  2194	     5	
  2195	     6	/**
  2196	     7	 * Operation-scoped mutation lock (plan §Concurrency, Lock model).
  2197	     8	 * Acquire = atomic non-recursive mkdir of .handoff/lock/; exclusivity comes from
  2198	     9	 * EEXIST. Ownership facts live in owner.json; logical session ownership lives in
  2199	    10	 * bundle.json, never here. Takeover only when the owner is provably dead on this
  2200	    11	 * host — every other contended state refuses, and `force` never changes that.
  2201	    12	 */
  2202	    13	
  2203	    14	export class LockHeldError extends Error {
  2204	    15	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
  2205	    16	    super(message);
  2206	    17	    this.name = 'LockHeldError';
  2207	    18	  }
  2208	    19	}
  2209	    20	
  2210	    21	export class FencingError extends Error {
  2211	    22	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
  2212	    23	    super(message);
  2213	    24	    this.name = 'FencingError';
  2214	    25	  }
  2215	    26	}
  2216	    27	
  2217	    28	/** @param {string} root */
  2218	    29	function lockPaths(root) {
  2219	    30	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
  2220	    31	  return {
  2221	    32	    dir,
  2222	    33	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
  2223	    34	    owner: `${dir}/lock/owner.json`,
  2224	    35	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
  2225	    36	  };
  2226	    37	}
  2227	    38	
  2228	    39	const ESCAPE_HATCH =
  2229	    40	  'Verify manually that no baton process still holds it, then delete .handoff/lock to clear.';
  2230	    41	
  2231	    42	/**
  2232	    43	 * Inspect the current lock state.
  2233	    44	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p
  2234	    45	 * @returns {{state: 'free'} | {state: 'torn', cause: string} | {state: 'live'|'dead'|'cross-host', owner: any}}
  2235	    46	 */
  2236	    47	function inspect(io, p) {
  2237	    48	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.lockDir)) return { state: 'free' };
  2238	    49	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.owner)) {
  2239	    50	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is missing (torn lock metadata)' };
  2240	    51	  }
  2241	    52	  let owner;
  2242	    53	  try {
  2243	    54	    owner = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
  2244	    55	  } catch {
  2245	    56	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is unparseable (torn lock metadata)' };
  2246	    57	  }
  2247	    58	  if (owner.host !== io.host) return { state: 'cross-host', owner };
  2248	    59	  if (io.processAlive(owner.pid, owner.startTime)) return { state: 'live', owner };
  2249	    60	  return { state: 'dead', owner };
  2250	    61	}
  2251	    62	
  2252	    63	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} text */
  2253	    64	function journalNote(io, p, text) {
  2254	    65	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
  2255	    66	  // Allocate seq + dedupeKey like every other journal entry (gate-2 fix,
  2256	    67	  // reviewer-b finding 2): a keyless, seq-less note poisons journalSeq to NaN
  2257	    68	  // on replay and dedupes every later keyless event. We are already inside the
  2258	    69	  // lock here, so reading the tail for allocation is race-free.
  2259	    70	  let tailSeq = 0;
  2260	    71	  try {
  2261	    72	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${p.dir}/bundle.json`, 'utf8'));
  2262	    73	    if (typeof snap?.journalSeq === 'number') tailSeq = snap.journalSeq;
  2263	    74	  } catch {
  2264	    75	    // no snapshot yet — journal tail below still counts
  2265	    76	  }
  2266	    77	  try {
  2267	    78	    for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
  2268	    79	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
  2269	    80	    }
  2270	    81	  } catch {
  2271	    82	    // no journal yet
  2272	    83	  }
  2273	    84	  const ts = io.now();
  2274	    85	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, {
  2275	    86	    seq: tailSeq + 1,
  2276	    87	    ts,
  2277	    88	    type: 'note',
  2278	    89	    dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts, type: 'note', source: 'lock', text }),
  2279	    90	    writerId: ['lock', io.host, io.pid].join('-'),
  2280	    91	    source: 'lock',
  2281	    92	    payload: { text },
  2282	    93	  });
  2283	    94	}
  2284	    95	
  2285	    96	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
  2286	    97	function publishOwner(io, p) {
  2287	    98	  const token = io.newFencingToken();
  2288	    99	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.owner, {
  2289	   100	    host: io.host,
  2290	   101	    pid: io.pid,
  2291	   102	    startTime: io.startTime,
  2292	   103	    fencingToken: token,
  2293	   104	    acquiredAt: io.now(),
  2294	   105	    heartbeatAt: io.now(),
  2295	   106	  });
  2296	   107	  return token;
  2297	   108	}
  2298	   109	
  2299	   110	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
  2300	   111	function acquire(io, p) {
  2301	   112	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
  2302	   113	  try {
  2303	   114	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
  2304	   115	    return publishOwner(io, p);
  2305	   116	  } catch (err) {
  2306	   117	    if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code !== 'EEXIST') throw err;
  2307	   118	  }
  2308	   119	
  2309	   120	  const s = inspect(io, p);
  2310	   121	  switch (s.state) {
  2311	   122	    case 'free':
  2312	   123	      // Raced a release between mkdir failure and inspection; retry once. A
  2313	   124	      // competitor can win THIS mkdir too (gate-2 fix 12: observed under real
  2314	   125	      // barrier-released contention) — that is contention, not corruption.
  2315	   126	      try {
  2316	   127	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
  2317	   128	      } catch (err) {
  2318	   129	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
  2319	   130	          throw new LockHeldError('lock was re-acquired by a competitor mid-inspection — transient contention');
  2320	   131	        }
  2321	   132	        throw err;
  2322	   133	      }
  2323	   134	      return publishOwner(io, p);
  2324	   135	    case 'live':
  2325	   136	      throw new LockHeldError(
  2326	   137	        `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
  2327	   138	      );
  2328	   139	    case 'cross-host':
  2329	   140	      throw new Error(
  2330	   141	        `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
  2331	   142	      );
  2332	   143	    case 'torn':
  2333	   144	      // During ACQUIRE, torn metadata is usually a competitor caught between
  2334	   145	      // its mkdir and owner.json publication — transient by construction, so
  2335	   146	      // LockHeldError lets withLock wait it out (gate-2 fix 12). A genuinely
  2336	   147	      // crashed publisher exhausts the retry budget and surfaces this same
  2337	   148	      // message; recovery semantics (recoverLock) are unchanged: never steal.
  2338	   149	      throw new LockHeldError(`${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`);
  2339	   150	    case 'dead': {
  2340	   151	      // Atomic dead-lock reclaim (gate-2 iter-2 B1, hardened iter-3 finding-1).
  2341	   152	      // rm-then-mkdir is NOT mutually exclusive: two contenders can both observe
  2342	   153	      // 'dead' before either removes the stale dir, then both rm+mkdir — the
  2343	   154	      // second silently clobbers the first, yielding two simultaneous holders and
  2344	   155	      // two takeover notes. rename() is the atomic arbiter instead: the stale
  2345	   156	      // lock dir has exactly one name, so only one process can move it aside (the
  2346	   157	      // rest get ENOENT). Winning the rename IS winning the reclaim; a contender
  2347	   158	      // that recreated the dir via the 'free' path first makes our mkdir EEXIST →
  2348	   159	      // transient contention. The fencing token stays the write-time backstop.
  2349	   160	      const aside = `${p.lockDir}.reclaim-${io.pid}-${io.newFencingToken()}`;
  2350	   161	      try {
  2351	   162	        io.fs.renameSync(p.lockDir, aside);
  2352	   163	      } catch (err) {
  2353	   164	        // Source already gone → another contender is reclaiming it; wait+retry.
  2354	   165	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'ENOENT') {
  2355	   166	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor is reclaiming the dead lock — transient contention');
  2356	   167	        }
  2357	   168	        throw err;
  2358	   169	      }
  2359	   170	      io.fs.rmSync(aside, { recursive: true, force: true });
  2360	   171	      try {
  2361	   172	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
  2362	   173	      } catch (err) {
  2363	   174	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
  2364	   175	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor re-claimed the dead lock first — transient contention');
  2365	   176	        }
  2366	   177	        throw err;
  2367	   178	      }
  2368	   179	      const token = publishOwner(io, p);
  2369	   180	      // Journal the takeover only now — we HOLD the lock (fresh dir + published
  2370	   181	      // owner), so the note's seq is allocated while holding it (plan
  2371	   182	      // §Concurrency "sequence numbers are allocated under the lock"), never
  2372	   183	      // racing a free-path competitor's concurrent append (iter-3 F2). The note
  2373	   184	      // is best-effort (0-or-1) and MUST NOT abort acquisition (iter-4 I7): if it
  2374	   185	      // threw here — after publishOwner, before the token is returned to withLock
  2375	   186	      // — the release finally would never run and the lock would leak with a live
  2376	   187	      // owner. Swallow it; the reclaim already succeeded.
  2377	   188	      try {
  2378	   189	        journalNote(io, p, `lock takeover: took over stale lock from dead pid ${s.owner.pid} (start time ${s.owner.startTime})`);
  2379	   190	      } catch {
  2380	   191	        // audit note is best-effort
  2381	   192	      }
  2382	   193	      return token;
  2383	   194	    }
  2384	   195	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
  2385	   196	    default:
  2386	   197	      throw new Error('unreachable lock state');
  2387	   198	  }
  2388	   199	}
  2389	   200	
  2390	   201	/**
  2391	   202	 * Fence-guarded release: the lock dir is removed only while owner.json still
  2392	   203	 * carries OUR token. A holder that paused past a takeover must not delete the
  2393	   204	 * competitor's lock on its way out — release is a write like any other.
  2394	   205	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} token
  2395	   206	 */
  2396	   207	function release(io, p, token) {
  2397	   208	  try {
  2398	   209	    const current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
  2399	   210	    if (current.fencingToken !== token) return; // a competitor took over — their lock, not ours
  2400	   211	  } catch {
  2401	   212	    return; // owner metadata gone or torn mid-hold — never guess; leave it for recovery
  2402	   213	  }
  2403	   214	  io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  2404	   215	}
  2405	   216	
  2406	   217	// A live same-host holder is transient by design — operation-scoped locks are
  2407	   218	// held for milliseconds — so contention waits instead of dropping the
  2408	   219	// operation (gate-2 fix 12: simultaneous checkpoints must all land). The budget
  2409	   220	// is generous (≈15 s): under heavy load a writer may be starved of scheduling
  2410	   221	// for seconds, and waiting is strictly safer than silently dropping its work.
  2411	   222	// Every other contended state (torn, cross-host) stays fail-fast: waiting
  2412	   223	// cannot make those provably safe.
  2413	   224	const DEFAULT_RETRY = { attempts: 600, delayMs: 25 };
  2414	   225	
  2415	   226	/** Synchronous sleep without burning a core: Atomics.wait on a throwaway buffer. */
  2416	   227	const sleepMs = (/** @type {number} */ ms) => {
  2417	   228	  Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);
  2418	   229	};
  2419	   230	
  2420	   231	/**
  2421	   232	 * Acquire → run fn(token) → release (also on throw). Synchronous by design:
  2422	   233	 * everything the lock guards is sync fs work. Live-held locks are retried on
  2423	   234	 * a bounded budget (override via io.lockRetry = {attempts, delayMs}).
  2424	   235	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {(token: string) => any} fn
  2425	   236	 */
  2426	   237	export function withLock(root, io, fn) {
  2427	   238	  const p = lockPaths(root);
  2428	   239	  // Jail BEFORE any lock mutation (gate-2 iter-2 B2): a symlinked .handoff or
  2429	   240	  // .handoff/lock must never be created into or removed through — that would
  2430	   241	  // reach outside the repository. Checked once here, ahead of acquire.
  2431	   242	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
  2432	   243	  if (!safe.ok) throw new Error(`${safe.problem} — refusing to acquire the lock`);
  2433	   244	  const retry = io.lockRetry ?? DEFAULT_RETRY;
  2434	   245	  /** @type {string} */
  2435	   246	  let token;
  2436	   247	  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
  2437	   248	    try {
  2438	   249	      token = acquire(io, p);
  2439	   250	      break;
  2440	   251	    } catch (err) {
  2441	   252	      if (err instanceof LockHeldError && attempt < retry.attempts) {
  2442	   253	        sleepMs(retry.delayMs);
  2443	   254	        continue;
  2444	   255	      }
  2445	   256	      throw err;
  2446	   257	    }
  2447	   258	  }
  2448	   259	  try {
  2449	   260	    return fn(token);
  2450	   261	  } finally {
  2451	   262	    release(io, p, token);
  2452	   263	  }
  2453	   264	}
  2454	   265	
  2455	   266	/**
  2456	   267	 * Fast-abort layer: re-read the published token immediately before a write; a
  2457	   268	 * holder that paused past its last check aborts instead of clobbering.
  2458	   269	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} token @param {() => any} fn
  2459	   270	 */
  2460	   271	export function guardedWrite(root, io, token, fn) {
  2461	   272	  const p = lockPaths(root);
  2462	   273	  let current = null;
  2463	   274	  try {
  2464	   275	    current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
  2465	   276	  } catch {
  2466	   277	    throw new FencingError('lock owner metadata is gone — fencing check failed; aborting write');
  2467	   278	  }
  2468	   279	  if (current.fencingToken !== token) {
  2469	   280	    throw new FencingError(
  2470	   281	      `fencing token changed (held '${token}', current '${current.fencingToken}') — another process took over; aborting write`,
  2471	   282	    );
  2472	   283	  }
  2473	   284	  return fn();
  2474	   285	}
  2475	   286	
  2476	   287	/**
  2477	   288	 * Explicit recovery. `force` is an intent flag only — it never changes the
  2478	   289	 * decision: exactly one state recovers (provably-dead same-host owner).
  2479	   290	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {{force?: boolean}} [opts]
  2480	   291	 * @returns {{recovered: boolean, refusedReason: string | null}}
  2481	   292	 */
  2482	   293	export function recoverLock(root, io, opts = {}) {
  2483	   294	  void opts;
  2484	   295	  const p = lockPaths(root);
  2485	   296	  // Jail before recovery (gate-2 iter-2 B2): recoverLock removes the lock dir,
  2486	   297	  // so a symlinked managed tree must be refused first — even with force, a
  2487	   298	  // symlink is never provably safe to delete through.
  2488	   299	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
  2489	   300	  if (!safe.ok) return { recovered: false, refusedReason: `${safe.problem} — refusing recovery` };
  2490	   301	  const s = inspect(io, p);
  2491	   302	  switch (s.state) {
  2492	   303	    case 'free':
  2493	   304	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
  2494	   305	    case 'dead': {
  2495	   306	      // Reclaim atomically via a single acquire() attempt + release, NOT an
  2496	   307	      // inspect-then-rmSync (iter-4 I1): the old bare rmSync had a TOCTOU — a
  2497	   308	      // competitor could win the rename-arbitrated dead-reclaim between this
  2498	   309	      // inspect() and the rmSync, publish a LIVE owner, and recovery would then
  2499	   310	      // delete a lock whose owner is alive. acquire() re-inspects atomically and
  2500	   311	      // shares the SAME rename arbitration, so recovery and a concurrent takeover
  2501	   312	      // are mutually exclusive: acquire either reclaims a still-dead lock (and
  2502	   313	      // journals the takeover under the lock) or throws because it is now
  2503	   314	      // live/torn — in which case we refuse rather than steal. release() then
  2504	   315	      // leaves the slot clear.
  2505	   316	      let token;
  2506	   317	      try {
  2507	   318	        token = acquire(io, p);
  2508	   319	      } catch (err) {
  2509	   320	        return { recovered: false, refusedReason: /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err) };
  2510	   321	      }
  2511	   322	      release(io, p, token);
  2512	   323	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
  2513	   324	    }
  2514	   325	    case 'live':
  2515	   326	      return {
  2516	   327	        recovered: false,
  2517	   328	        refusedReason: `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
  2518	   329	      };
  2519	   330	    case 'cross-host':
  2520	   331	      return {
  2521	   332	        recovered: false,
  2522	   333	        refusedReason: `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
  2523	   334	      };
  2524	   335	    case 'torn':
  2525	   336	      return {
  2526	   337	        recovered: false,
  2527	   338	        refusedReason: `${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
  2528	   339	      };
  2529	   340	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
  2530	   341	    default:
  2531	   342	      return { recovered: false, refusedReason: 'unreachable lock state' };
  2532	   343	  }
  2533	   344	}

  2542	     1	import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
  2543	     2	
  2544	     3	// Git facts degrade to null rather than block a checkpoint: not-a-repo,
  2545	     4	// missing binary, and timeouts are all expected environments (plan §Root
  2546	     5	// discovery & degraded modes).
  2547	     6	const GIT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000;
  2548	     7	// The untracked digest is bounded: beyond this many paths, entries fold in by
  2549	     8	// name only, keeping prepare-time hashing cheap on huge untracked trees.
  2550	     9	const UNTRACKED_CAP = 200;
  2551	    10	// NUL separator: cannot occur in diffs, sizes, hex hashes, or file paths, so
  2552	    11	// joined digest inputs cannot collide across field boundaries.
  2553	    12	const SEP = String.fromCharCode(0);
  2554	    13	const NEWLINE = String.fromCharCode(10);
  2555	    14	
  2556	    15	const sha256 = (/** @type {string} */ s) => createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
  2557	    16	
  2558	    17	/**
  2559	    18	 * Capture the repo's git state plus a content-sensitive digest. The digest
  2560	    19	 * covers the staged diff, unstaged diff, and a bounded untracked-files digest
  2561	    20	 * (path + size + content hash) — NOT the HEAD sha, which the receive token
  2562	    21	 * binds as its own input, so a HEAD-only move is distinguishable from a
  2563	    22	 * content change.
  2564	    23	 * @param {{execFile: Function, cwd: string, fs: any}} deps
  2565	    24	 * @returns {Promise<{branch: string, headSha: string, dirty: boolean, dirtySummary: string[], contentDigest: string} | null>}
  2566	    25	 */
  2567	    26	export async function snapshot({ execFile, cwd, fs }) {
  2568	    27	  const git = (/** @type {string[]} */ args) => execFile('git', args, { cwd, timeout: GIT_TIMEOUT_MS });
  2569	    28	  let branch, head, status, staged, unstaged, untrackedList;
  2570	    29	  try {
  2571	    30	    [branch, head, status, staged, unstaged, untrackedList] = await Promise.all([
  2572	    31	      git(['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD']),
  2573	    32	      git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']),
  2574	    33	      git(['status', '--porcelain']),
  2575	    34	      git(['diff', '--cached']),
  2576	    35	      git(['diff']),
  2577	    36	      git(['ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard']),
  2578	    37	    ]);
  2579	    38	  } catch {
  2580	    39	    return null;
  2581	    40	  }
  2582	    41	
  2583	    42	  const nonEmptyLines = (/** @type {string} */ s) => s.split(NEWLINE).filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l !== '');
  2584	    43	  const dirtySummary = nonEmptyLines(status.stdout);
  2585	    44	  const untrackedPaths = nonEmptyLines(untrackedList.stdout);
  2586	    45	
  2587	    46	  const untrackedEntries = untrackedPaths.map((rel, i) => {
  2588	    47	    if (i >= UNTRACKED_CAP) return [rel, 'capped'].join(SEP);
  2589	    48	    try {
  2590	    49	      const content = fs.readFileSync(cwd + '/' + rel, 'utf8');
  2591	    50	      return [rel, String(Buffer.byteLength(content)), sha256(content)].join(SEP);
  2592	    51	    } catch {
  2593	    52	      // Listed but unreadable (deleted mid-flight): still digest its presence.
  2594	    53	      return [rel, 'absent'].join(SEP);
  2595	    54	    }
  2596	    55	  });
  2597	    56	
  2598	    57	  return {
  2599	    58	    branch: branch.stdout.trim(),
  2600	    59	    headSha: head.stdout.trim(),
  2601	    60	    dirty: dirtySummary.length > 0,
  2602	    61	    dirtySummary,
  2603	    62	    contentDigest: sha256([staged.stdout, unstaged.stdout, ...untrackedEntries].join(SEP)),
  2604	    63	  };
  2605	    64	}

  2695	    88	/** Event-shaped: an object carrying a string `type`. */
  2696	    89	const isEventShaped = (/** @type {any} */ v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) && typeof v.type === 'string';
  2697	    90	
  2698	    91	/**
  2699	    92	 * Tolerant stdin parse (live /baton:handoff failure: a model sent one event
  2700	    93	 * per line and the whole narrative checkpoint silently failed). Accept the
  2701	    94	 * canonical single JSON value, a bare array of events, or NDJSON where every
  2702	    95	 * line is an event object or a {schema, events} wrapper. All-or-nothing: one
  2703	    96	 * non-event element fails the whole parse — never a partial apply.
  2704	    97	 * @param {string} text @returns {{ok: true, raw: any} | {ok: false, why?: string}}
  2705	    98	 */
  2706	    99	function parseStdin(text) {
  2707	   100	  let single;
  2708	   101	  let singleOk = false;
  2709	   102	  try {
  2710	   103	    single = JSON.parse(text);
  2711	   104	    singleOk = true;
  2712	   105	  } catch {
  2713	   106	    singleOk = false;
  2714	   107	  }
  2715	   108	  if (singleOk) {
  2716	   109	    if (Array.isArray(single)) {
  2717	   110	      return single.every(isEventShaped) ? { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events: single } } : { ok: false };
  2718	   111	    }
  2719	   112	    // A closest-miss of the documented wrapper — an events array whose schema
  2720	   113	    // key is missing or wrong — must ERROR, never silently degrade to a junk
  2721	   114	    // note with ok:true (the model cannot self-correct from a success
  2722	   115	    // envelope while its narrative was discarded).
  2723	   116	    if (single !== null && typeof single === 'object' && Array.isArray(single.events) && single.schema !== 'baton/event@1') {
  2724	   117	      return { ok: false, why: `stdin carries an events array but schema is ${single.schema === undefined ? 'missing' : JSON.stringify(single.schema)} — use {"schema":"baton/event@1","events":[…]}` };
  2725	   118	    }
  2726	   119	    // A single bare event object (string `type`, none of the raw-hook payload
  2727	   120	    // markers) is one of the two most likely readings of "pipe events on
  2728	   121	    // stdin" — accept it as that event instead of degrading it to a junk note.
  2729	   122	    if (
  2730	   123	      isEventShaped(single) &&
  2731	   124	      single.schema === undefined &&
  2732	   125	      !('hook_event_name' in single) &&
  2733	   126	      !('session_id' in single) &&
  2734	   127	      !('event' in single)
  2735	   128	    ) {
  2736	   129	      return { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events: [single] } };
  2737	   130	    }
  2738	   131	    return { ok: true, raw: single };
  2739	   132	  }
  2740	   133	  const lines = text.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l !== '');
  2741	   134	  if (lines.length === 0) return { ok: false };
  2742	   135	  /** @type {any[]} */
  2743	   136	  const events = [];
  2744	   137	  for (const line of lines) {
  2745	   138	    let v;
  2746	   139	    try {
  2747	   140	      v = JSON.parse(line);
  2748	   141	    } catch {
  2749	   142	      return { ok: false };
  2750	   143	    }
  2751	   144	    if (v && typeof v === 'object' && v.schema === 'baton/event@1' && Array.isArray(v.events)) {
  2752	   145	      if (!v.events.every(isEventShaped)) return { ok: false };
  2753	   146	      events.push(...v.events);
  2754	   147	    } else if (isEventShaped(v)) {
  2755	   148	      events.push(v);
  2756	   149	    } else {
  2757	   150	      return { ok: false };
  2758	   151	    }
  2759	   152	  }
  2760	   153	  return { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events } };
  2761	   154	}
  2762	   155	
  2763	   156	/**
  2764	   157	 * `baton checkpoint` — mechanical checkpoint from a hook payload on stdin.
  2765	   158	 * Hook-safety rule: exit 0 on every soft failure so a checkpoint can never
  2766	   159	 * break the host harness; `--strict` opts into hard failures (CI).
  2767	   160	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
  2768	   161	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  2769	   162	 */
  2770	   163	export async function cmdCheckpoint(args, io) {
  2771	   164	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', model: 'string', trigger: 'string', debounce: 'string', strict: 'boolean', 'take-over': 'boolean' });
  2772	   165	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'checkpoint', parsed.error);
  2773	   166	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  2774	   167	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
  2775	   168	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
  2776	   169	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
  2777	   170	  const pErr = platformError(platform);
  2778	   171	  if (pErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', pErr);
  2779	   172	
  2780	   173	  try {
  2781	   174	    return await run(flags, platform, io);
  2782	   175	  } catch (err) {
  2783	   176	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
  2784	   177	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'checkpoint-failed', msg } });
  2785	   178	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${msg}\n`);
  2786	   179	    return strict ? 1 : 0;
  2787	   180	  }
  2788	   181	}
  2789	   182	
  2790	   183	/**
  2791	   184	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
  2792	   185	 * @param {string} platform
  2793	   186	 * @param {any} io
  2794	   187	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  2795	   188	 */
  2796	   189	async function run(flags, platform, io) {
  2797	   190	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
  2798	   191	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  2799	   192	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
  2800	   193	  // The --json contract (gate-2 fix 11): exactly one envelope on stdout for
  2801	   194	  // every exit path — the exit code stays governed by hook-safety.
  2802	   195	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ env, /** @type {number} */ code) => {
  2803	   196	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, env);
  2804	   197	    return code;
  2805	   198	  };
  2806	   199	
  2807	   200	  const parsed = parseStdin(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
  2808	   201	  if (!parsed.ok) {
  2809	   202	    const why = parsed.why ?? 'send one JSON value ({"schema":"baton/event@1","events":[…]}), a JSON array of events, or NDJSON event lines';
  2810	   203	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: stdin could not be parsed — ${why}\n`);
  2811	   204	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-stdin', msg: why } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
  2812	   205	  }
  2813	   206	  const raw = parsed.raw;
  2814	   207	
  2815	   208	  const session = { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime };
  2816	   209	  // The hook command declares which event it is (--trigger), so event identity
  2817	   210	  // never depends on parsing each harness's payload shape (Cursor's stop payload
  2818	   211	  // names the event nowhere normalize could find it).
  2819	   212	  const explicitEvent = typeof flags.trigger === 'string' ? flags.trigger : undefined;
  2820	   213	  const events = normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session, explicitEvent);
  2821	   214	  if (events.length === 0) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false } }, 0);
  2822	   215	
  2823	   216	  // Opt-in transcript tail (gate-2 fix 10): rides the journal as its own
  2824	   217	  // IMPORTANT event so the snapshot rewrite below persists it, and purge can
  2825	   218	  // strip it from every retained copy by its property name.
  2826	   219	  const tail = captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io);
  2827	   220	  if (tail !== null) {
  2828	   221	    events.push({
  2829	   222	      type: 'transcript.set',
  2830	   223	      payload: { transcript: { capturedAt: io.now(), tail } },
  2831	   224	      source: platform,
  2832	   225	      sessionHint: events[0]?.sessionHint,
  2833	   226	      unstable: events[0]?.unstable ?? true,
  2834	   227	    });
  2835	   228	  }
  2836	   229	
  2837	   230	  const { bundle, warnings, unsafe } = loadBundle(root, io);
  2838	   231	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${w}\n`);
  2839	   232	
  2840	   233	  // An unsafe managed tree (symlinked .handoff — gate-2 fix 6) is NOT "no
  2841	   234	  // bundle yet": seeding here would write through the link. Refuse everything;
  2842	   235	  // hook-safety keeps the refusal soft outside --strict.
  2843	   236	  if (unsafe === true) {
  2844	   237	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: managed tree failed the symlink/realpath jail — nothing written\n');
  2845	   238	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'unsafe-tree', msg: warnings[0] ?? 'managed tree failed the jail check' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
  2846	   239	  }
  2847	   240	
  2848	   241	  // Opt-in debounce (gate-2 major 14; Cursor afterFileEdit fires per edit):
  2849	   242	  // at most one real checkpoint per window, tracked by a per-platform stamp.
  2850	   243	  // The read-and-set runs UNDER the repo lock (gate-2 iter-2 M5) so two
  2851	   244	  // simultaneous afterFileEdit hooks can't both observe no stamp and both

  2978	    27	/**
  2979	    28	 * Repo-root discovery (plan §Root discovery): `--root` wins; else the nearest
  2980	    29	 * ancestor of io.cwd carrying `.handoff/` or `baton.config.json`; else the
  2981	    30	 * nearest ancestor carrying `.git` (the repo toplevel — and a boundary the
  2982	    31	 * walk never crosses, so a nested repo resolves to itself); else io.cwd.
  2983	    32	 * @param {any} io @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
  2984	    33	 * @returns {string}
  2985	    34	 */
  2986	    35	export function resolveRoot(io, flags) {
  2987	    36	  if (typeof flags.root === 'string' && flags.root.length > 0) return flags.root;
  2988	    37	  // Normalize separators up front (gate-2 iter-2 M2): a Windows cwd arrives
  2989	    38	  // backslash-separated, but Node fs accepts forward slashes, so the ascent
  2990	    39	  // and the marker checks work in one forward-slash space. The last separator
  2991	    40	  // is either kind; the loop stops at a drive/UNC or filesystem root.
  2992	    41	  const start = normSep(io.cwd);
  2993	    42	  let dir = start;
  2994	    43	  while (true) {
  2995	    44	    try {
  2996	    45	      if (io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, '.handoff')) || io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, 'baton.config.json'))) return dir;
  2997	    46	      if (io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, '.git'))) return dir; // repo toplevel; never walk past a .git boundary
  2998	    47	    } catch {
  2999	    48	      break;
  3000	    49	    }
  3001	    50	    const cut = dir.lastIndexOf('/');
  3002	    51	    if (cut < 0) break;
  3003	    52	    const parent = cut === 0 ? '/' : dir.slice(0, cut);
  3004	    53	    // At a drive-relative ceiling ("C:") examine the drive ROOT ("C:/") once —
  3005	    54	    // a repo can live directly at C:/ (iter-3 F6) — then stop.
  3006	    55	    if (/^[A-Za-z]:$/.test(parent)) {
  3007	    56	      const driveRoot = `${parent}/`;
  3008	    57	      if (driveRoot !== dir) {
  3009	    58	        dir = driveRoot;
  3010	    59	        continue;
  3011	    60	      }
  3012	    61	      break;
  3013	    62	    }
  3014	    63	    if (parent === dir) break;
  3015	    64	    dir = parent;
  3016	    65	  }
  3017	    66	  return start;
  3018	    67	}

  3026	    75	 * rejected the REAL platform's checkpoints as foreign.
  3027	    76	 * @param {string | boolean | undefined} value

  3029	    78	 */
  3030	    79	export function platformError(value) {

  3032	    81	  if (typeof value === 'string' && PLATFORMS.includes(value)) return null;
