  3035	    84	
  3036	    85	/**
  3037	    86	 * Strict flag parser (surface-audit fold; docs/design/core.md §tooling always
  3038	    87	 * specified strict parsing). spec maps each flag name to 'string' | 'boolean';
  3039	    88	 * `json` and `root` are implied common flags. Errors — unknown flag, stray
  3040	    89	 * positional, missing string-flag value — return {error} so the command exits
  3041	    90	 * 2 instead of silently losing intent: the lenient parser let a stray token
  3042	    91	 * flip SAFETY flags off (checkpoint/doctor --strict, remap --native-only,
  3043	    92	 * receive --print-prompt all read fail-dangerous exit 0).
  3044	    93	 * @param {string[]} args
  3045	    94	 * @param {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} spec
  3046	    95	 * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, error?: string}}
  3047	    96	 */
  3048	    97	export function parseFlagsStrict(args, spec) {
  3049	    98	  /** @type {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} */
  3050	    99	  const full = { json: 'boolean', root: 'string', ...spec };
  3051	   100	  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
  3052	   101	  const flags = {};
  3053	   102	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  3054	   103	    const a = args[i];
  3055	   104	    if (!a.startsWith('--')) return { flags, error: `unexpected argument '${a}' — flags are --name [value]` };
  3056	   105	    const key = a.slice(2);
  3057	   106	    const kind = full[key];
  3058	   107	    if (kind === undefined) return { flags, error: `unknown flag --${key}` };
  3059	   108	    if (kind === 'boolean') {
  3060	   109	      // A following token that is not a flag would have been this flag's
  3061	   110	      // silent "value" under the lenient parser; reject it loudly.
  3062	   111	      const next = args[i + 1];
  3063	   112	      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
  3064	   113	        return { flags, error: `unexpected token '${next}' after --${key} (a bare flag takes no value)` };
  3065	   114	      }
  3066	   115	      flags[key] = true;
  3067	   116	      continue;
  3068	   117	    }
  3069	   118	    const next = args[i + 1];
  3070	   119	    // A string flag consumes the next token unless it is itself a KNOWN flag
  3071	   120	    // (so `--commit --origin x` errors instead of eating '--origin').
  3072	   121	    if (next === undefined || (next.startsWith('--') && full[next.slice(2)] !== undefined)) {
  3073	   122	      return { flags, error: `--${key} requires a value` };
  3074	   123	    }
  3075	   124	    flags[key] = next;
  3076	   125	    i += 1;
  3077	   126	  }
  3078	   127	  return { flags };
  3079	   128	}
  3080	   129	
  3081	   130	/**
  3082	   131	 * Minimal flag parser: `--key value` pairs (value = next token not starting
  3083	   132	 * with --), bare `--key` booleans, positionals collected in order.
  3084	   133	 * @param {string[]} args
  3085	   134	 * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, positionals: string[]}}
  3086	   135	 */
  3087	   136	export function parseFlags(args) {
  3088	   137	  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
  3089	   138	  const flags = {};
  3090	   139	  /** @type {string[]} */
  3091	   140	  const positionals = [];
  3092	   141	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  3093	   142	    const a = args[i];
  3094	   143	    if (a.startsWith('--')) {
  3095	   144	      const key = a.slice(2);
  3096	   145	      const next = args[i + 1];
  3097	   146	      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
  3098	   147	        flags[key] = next;
  3099	   148	        i += 1;
  3100	   149	      } else {
  3101	   150	        flags[key] = true;
  3102	   151	      }
  3103	   152	    } else {
  3104	   153	      positionals.push(a);
  3105	   154	    }
  3106	   155	  }
  3107	   156	  return { flags, positionals };
  3108	   157	}

  3255	   143	
  3256	   144	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
  3257	   145	}

  3548	    35	/** @param {any} text @returns {string | null} */
  3549	    36	const parseVersion = (text) => String(text).match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)+/)?.[0] ?? null;
  3550	    37	
  3551	    38	/** Numeric segment-wise version compare: negative when a < b. */
  3552	    39	function cmpVersions(/** @type {string} */ a, /** @type {string} */ b) {
  3553	    40	  const as = a.split('.').map(Number);
  3554	    41	  const bs = b.split('.').map(Number);
  3555	    42	  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(as.length, bs.length); i++) {
  3556	    43	    const d = (as[i] ?? 0) - (bs[i] ?? 0);
  3557	    44	    if (d !== 0) return d < 0 ? -1 : 1;
  3558	    45	  }
  3559	    46	  return 0;
  3560	    47	}

  3562	    49	/** @param {string} platform @param {any} io @returns {Promise<string | null>} */
  3563	    50	async function versionOf(platform, io) {
  3564	    51	  try {
  3565	    52	    const r = await io.execFile(VERSION_BINS[platform], ['--version'], { timeout: PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS });
  3566	    53	    return parseVersion(`${r.stdout ?? ''} ${r.stderr ?? ''}`);
  3567	    54	  } catch {
  3568	    55	    return null;
  3569	    56	  }
  3570	    57	}

  3572	    59	/** @param {string} platform @param {string | null} v @returns {{id: string, ok: boolean, detail: string}} */
  3573	    60	function versionCheck(platform, v) {
  3574	    61	  const id = `version-${platform}`;
  3575	    62	  if (v === null) {
  3576	    63	    return { id, ok: true, detail: `${VERSION_BINS[platform]} version unverifiable (not installed or --version unsupported) — floor not enforced` };
  3577	    64	  }
  3578	    65	  const f = FLOORS[platform];
  3579	    66	  if (f.floor && cmpVersions(v, f.floor) < 0) {
  3580	    67	    return { id, ok: false, detail: `${platform} ${v} is below the supported floor ${f.label} — lifecycle hooks unavailable; the adapter degrades to protocol-only (Tier C)` };
  3581	    68	  }
  3582	    69	  if (cmpVersions(v, TESTED[platform]) > 0) {
  3583	    70	    return { id, ok: true, detail: `${platform} ${v} is newer than tested (${TESTED[platform]}) — untested; signature drift is absorbed by the overlay table` };
  3584	    71	  }
  3585	    72	  return { id, ok: true, detail: `${platform} ${v} (tested ${TESTED[platform]})` };
  3586	    73	}

  3588	    75	/**
  3589	    76	 * Recursively collect every `command`/`commandWindows` STRING under a hook
  3590	    77	 * definition (codex nests hooks[].hooks[].command; cursor is [].command). Only
  3591	    78	 * real command fields — never matchers, comments, or serialized metadata — so
  3592	    79	 * enablement keys on an actual invocation (iter-5 A3).
  3593	    80	 * @param {any} def @returns {string[]}
  3594	    81	 */
  3595	    82	function hookCommands(def) {
  3596	    83	  /** @type {string[]} */
  3597	    84	  const out = [];
  3598	    85	  const walk = (/** @type {any} */ node) => {
  3599	    86	    if (Array.isArray(node)) {
  3600	    87	      for (const n of node) walk(n);
  3601	    88	    } else if (node && typeof node === 'object') {
  3602	    89	      if (typeof node.command === 'string') out.push(node.command);
  3603	    90	      if (typeof node.commandWindows === 'string') out.push(node.commandWindows);
  3604	    91	      if (Array.isArray(node.hooks)) walk(node.hooks);
  3605	    92	    }
  3606	    93	  };
  3607	    94	  walk(def);
  3608	    95	  return out;
  3609	    96	}
  3610	    97	
  3611	    98	/**
  3612	    99	 * Codex/Cursor hook surface: installed / enabled / trusted / observed-executing.
  3613	   100	 * `installed` = the hooks file references baton at all; `enabled` = the
  3614	   101	 * mechanical-checkpoint hook (the `checkpointEvent` key: codex `Stop`, cursor
  3615	   102	 * `stop`) declares a `baton checkpoint` COMMAND (iter-5 A3); `trusted` is not
  3616	   103	 * externally verifiable → 'unknown'; `observed` = a platform-sourced checkpoint
  3617	   104	 * whose payload.trigger is the checkpointEvent (the per-turn Stop canary). The
  3618	   105	 * ≤1-turn fidelity claim is gated on enabled AND observed.
  3619	   106	 * @param {any} io @param {string} root @param {any} p bundlePaths
  3620	   107	 * @param {string} platform @param {string} relPath @param {string} checkpointEvent @param {string} trustNote
  3621	   108	 */
  3622	   109	function hookSurfaceCheck(io, root, p, platform, relPath, checkpointEvent, trustNote) {
  3623	   110	  const id = `${platform}-hooks`;
  3624	   111	  let text = null;
  3625	   112	  try {
  3626	   113	    text = io.fs.readFileSync(`${root}/${relPath}`, 'utf8');
  3627	   114	  } catch {
  3628	   115	    text = null;
  3629	   116	  }
  3630	   117	  const installed = text !== null && text.includes('baton');
  3631	   118	  if (!installed) {
  3632	   119	    return {
  3633	   120	      id,
  3634	   121	      ok: true,
  3635	   122	      states: { installed: false, enabled: false, trusted: 'unknown', observed: false },
  3636	   123	      detail: `not installed — run 'baton init --${platform}' to write ${relPath}`,
  3637	   124	    };
  3638	   125	  }
  3639	   126	  // enabled: the checkpoint hook itself is declared with a `baton checkpoint`
  3640	   127	  // COMMAND (iter-5 A3) — inspect the actual command/commandWindows fields, not
  3641	   128	  // the serialized blob (a matcher/comment mentioning baton must not count). A
  3642	   129	  // file that references baton only in a session-start hook is installed but NOT
  3643	   130	  // checkpoint-enabled.
  3644	   131	  let enabled = false;
  3645	   132	  try {
  3646	   133	    const cfg = JSON.parse(/** @type {string} */ (text));
  3647	   134	    // Match the bare `baton checkpoint` form AND the absolute
  3648	   135	    // `"<node>" "<…/baton.mjs>" checkpoint` form init now writes (the entry
  3649	   136	    // script may be quote-terminated: `baton.mjs" checkpoint`).
  3650	   137	    enabled = hookCommands(cfg?.hooks?.[checkpointEvent]).some((c) => /\bbaton(?:\.mjs)?["']?\s+checkpoint\b/.test(c));
  3651	   138	  } catch {
  3652	   139	    enabled = false;
  3653	   140	  }
  3654	   141	  // observed: a mechanical checkpoint from THIS platform's CHECKPOINT hook is
  3655	   142	  // recorded — the journal entry must be platform-sourced AND carry
  3656	   143	  // payload.trigger === checkpointEvent (iter-5 A3). normalize stamps the hook
  3657	   144	  // event name as payload.trigger, so a SessionStart/PreCompact/triggerless
  3658	   145	  // entry no longer masquerades as the per-turn Stop canary.
  3659	   146	  let observed = false;
  3660	   147	  try {
  3661	   148	    for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
  3662	   149	      const mine = String(e?.writerId ?? '').startsWith(`${platform}-`) || e?.source === platform;
  3663	   150	      if (mine && e?.payload?.trigger === checkpointEvent) {
  3664	   151	        observed = true;
  3665	   152	        break;
  3666	   153	      }
  3667	   154	    }
  3668	   155	  } catch {
  3669	   156	    observed = false;
  3670	   157	  }
  3671	   158	  const states = { installed: true, enabled, trusted: 'unknown', observed };
  3672	   159	  // The ≤1-turn claim needs the checkpoint hook DECLARED (enabled) AND OBSERVED —
  3673	   160	  // neither alone suffices.
  3674	   161	  const fidelityHolds = enabled && observed;
  3675	   162	  const enabledNote = enabled ? `${checkpointEvent} checkpoint hook enabled` : `${checkpointEvent} checkpoint hook NOT declared (enabled=false)`;
  3676	   163	  return {
  3677	   164	    id,
  3678	   165	    ok: true,
  3679	   166	    states,
  3680	   167	    detail: fidelityHolds
  3681	   168	      ? `installed, ${enabledNote}; trusted=unknown (not externally verifiable); execution OBSERVED (canary) — the ≤1-turn mechanical-staleness claim holds`
  3682	   169	      : `installed, ${enabledNote}; trusted=unknown — ${trustNote}; ${observed ? 'execution observed but' : 'execution not yet observed —'} the ≤1-turn fidelity claim is gated on the ${checkpointEvent} canary + enablement`,
  3683	   170	  };
  3684	   171	}
  3685	   172	
  3686	   173	// Noninteractive status probes per platform (docs/design/platform-notes.md CLIs).
  3687	   174	// capOnSuccess caps what a clean exit PROVES (gate-2 iter-2 M3): `claude
  3688	   175	// --version` proves the binary is INSTALLED, not that the server was reached or
  3689	   176	// that auth is valid, so its capability is capped at "installed". The codex/
  3690	   177	// cursor probes are auth-checking status commands, so a clean exit legitimately
  3691	   178	// proves "reachable".
  3692	   179	const PROBES = [
  3693	   180	  { platform: 'claude-code', bin: 'claude', args: ['--version'], capOnSuccess: 'installed' },
  3694	   181	  { platform: 'codex', bin: 'codex', args: ['login', 'status'], capOnSuccess: 'reachable' },
  3695	   182	  { platform: 'cursor', bin: 'cursor-agent', args: ['status'], capOnSuccess: 'reachable' },
  3696	   183	];
  3697	   184	
  3698	   185	// Word-boundary-safe AI-attribution trailer patterns (plan §Attribution guard).
  3699	   186	const TRAILER_PATTERNS = [
  3700	   187	  /co-authored-by:[^\n]*\b(claude|gpt|copilot|codex)\b/i,
  3701	   188	  /generated (with|by)[^\n]*\bclaude\b/i,
  3702	   189	  /\u{1F916}/u,
  3703	   190	];
  3704	   191	
  3705	   192	/** @param {any} io @param {string} path @returns {string | null} */
  3706	   193	function readOrNull(io, path) {
  3707	   194	  try {
  3708	   195	    return io.fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
  3709	   196	  } catch {
  3710	   197	    return null;
  3711	   198	  }
  3712	   199	}
  3713	   200	
  3714	   201	/**
  3715	   202	 * Classify one probe invocation per the two-dimension record: capability is
  3716	   203	 * the ordered ladder verified as far as the evidence goes; outcome is what the
  3717	   204	 * probe itself did. A server-issued limit response proves authentication.
  3718	   205	 * @param {{platform: string, bin: string, args: string[], capOnSuccess?: string}} probe @param {any} io @param {any} table
  3719	   206	 */
  3720	   207	async function runProbe(probe, io, table) {
  3721	   208	  try {
  3722	   209	    await io.execFile(probe.bin, probe.args, { timeout: PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS });
  3723	   210	    return { platform: probe.platform, capability: probe.capOnSuccess ?? 'reachable', outcome: 'ok' };
  3724	   211	  } catch (err) {
  3725	   212	    const e = /** @type {any} */ (err);
  3726	   213	    if (e?.code === 'ENOENT') return { platform: probe.platform, capability: null, outcome: 'error' };
  3727	   214	    if (e?.killed === true) return { platform: probe.platform, capability: 'installed', outcome: 'timeout' };
  3728	   215	    const text = `${e?.stdout ?? ''}\n${e?.stderr ?? ''}`;
  3729	   216	    if (table && classify({ text, exitCode: 1, platform: probe.platform, table, structured: null }).class === 'usage-limit') {
  3730	   217	      return { platform: probe.platform, capability: 'authenticated', outcome: 'rate-limited' };
  3731	   218	    }
  3732	   219	    if (/authenticated as|logged in as/i.test(text) && /unreachable|ECONN|offline|network/i.test(text)) {
  3733	   220	      return { platform: probe.platform, capability: 'authenticated', outcome: 'error' };
  3734	   221	    }
  3735	   222	    if (/not logged in|log ?in first|unauthorized|authenticat/i.test(text)) {
  3736	   223	      return { platform: probe.platform, capability: 'installed', outcome: 'error' };
  3737	   224	    }
  3738	   225	    return { platform: probe.platform, capability: 'installed', outcome: 'error' };
  3739	   226	  }
  3740	   227	}
  3741	   228	
  3742	   229	/** @param {any} io @param {string} root @returns {Promise<{id: string, ok: boolean, detail?: string}>} */
  3743	   230	async function gitGuardCheck(io, root) {
  3744	   231	  let log;
  3745	   232	  try {
  3746	   233	    log = await io.execFile('git', ['log', '-n', String(GUARD_COMMIT_BOUND), '--format=%h %s%n%n%b'], { cwd: root, timeout: 5000 });
  3747	   234	  } catch {
  3748	   235	    return { id: 'git-guard', ok: true, detail: 'git unavailable — commit scan skipped' };
  3749	   236	  }
  3750	   237	  // Chunks start at a line beginning with an abbreviated sha + space.
  3751	   238	  const chunks = log.stdout.split(/^(?=[0-9a-f]{7,40} )/m).filter((/** @type {string} */ c) => c.trim() !== '');
  3752	   239	  for (const chunk of chunks) {
  3753	   240	    if (TRAILER_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(chunk))) {
  3754	   241	      const sha = chunk.slice(0, chunk.indexOf(' '));
  3755	   242	      return {
  3756	   243	        id: 'git-guard',
  3757	   244	        ok: false,
  3758	   245	        detail: `commit ${sha} carries an AI attribution trailer — rewrite it before publishing (git rebase -i / git commit --amend)`,
  3759	   246	      };
  3760	   247	    }
  3761	   248	  }
  3762	   249	  return { id: 'git-guard', ok: true, detail: `no AI trailers in the last ${GUARD_COMMIT_BOUND} commits` };
  3763	   250	}
  3764	   251	
  3765	   252	/**
  3766	   253	 * `baton doctor` — machine health report: attribution settings, git-guard
  3767	   254	 * trailer scan, journal size, filesystem boundary, and per-platform probe
  3768	   255	 * records ({capability, outcome}, 3 s bound, 15-min cache). Report-only by
  3769	   256	 * default; `--strict` turns any failing check into exit 1.
  3770	   257	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
  3771	   258	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  3772	   259	 */
  3773	   260	export async function cmdDoctor(args, io) {
  3774	   261	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { strict: 'boolean' });
  3775	   262	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'doctor', parsed.error);
  3776	   263	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  3777	   264	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  3778	   265	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  3779	   266	  /** @type {{id: string, ok: boolean, detail?: string}[]} */
  3780	   267	  const checks = [];
  3781	   268	
  3782	   269	  // Attribution settings: repo-level first, then the user's global file.
  3783	   270	  const settingsText = readOrNull(io, `${root}/.claude/settings.json`) ?? readOrNull(io, `${io.env?.HOME ?? ''}/.claude/settings.json`);
  3784	   271	  let attrOk = false;
  3785	   272	  try {
  3786	   273	    const s = settingsText === null ? null : JSON.parse(settingsText);
  3787	   274	    attrOk = s?.attribution?.commit === '' && s?.attribution?.pr === '';
  3788	   275	  } catch {
  3789	   276	    attrOk = false;
  3790	   277	  }
  3791	   278	  checks.push({
  3792	   279	    id: 'attribution-settings',
  3793	   280	    ok: attrOk,
  3794	   281	    detail: attrOk ? 'attribution.commit and attribution.pr are ""' : 'set {"attribution":{"commit":"","pr":""}} in .claude/settings.json (baton init does this)',
  3795	   282	  });
  3796	   283	
  3797	   284	  checks.push(await gitGuardCheck(io, root));
  3798	   285	
  3799	   286	  // .handoff gitignore coverage (audit finding): doctor itself writes the
  3800	   287	  // probe cache into .handoff/log/, so running doctor BEFORE init in a clean
  3801	   288	  // repo leaves .handoff/ committable. Flag the gap whenever the tree exists
  3802	   289	  // without ignore coverage.
  3803	   290	  {
  3804	   291	    let handoffExists = false;
  3805	   292	    try {
  3806	   293	      handoffExists = io.fs.statSync(`${root}/.handoff`).isDirectory();
  3807	   294	    } catch {
  3808	   295	      handoffExists = false;
  3809	   296	    }
  3810	   297	    const covered = !ensureIgnoreLine(readOrNull(io, `${root}/.gitignore`), '.handoff/').changed;
  3811	   298	    checks.push({
  3812	   299	      id: 'handoff-ignored',
  3813	   300	      ok: !handoffExists || covered,
  3814	   301	      detail:
  3815	   302	        !handoffExists || covered
  3816	   303	          ? handoffExists
  3817	   304	            ? '.gitignore covers .handoff/'
  3818	   305	            : 'no .handoff/ tree yet'
  3819	   306	          : '.handoff/ exists but .gitignore does not cover it — run baton init (never commit .handoff/)',
  3820	   307	    });
  3821	   308	  }
  3822	   309	
  3823	   310	  const journalText = readOrNull(io, p.journal);
  3824	   311	  const journalBytes = journalText === null ? 0 : Buffer.byteLength(journalText);
  3825	   312	  checks.push({
  3826	   313	    id: 'journal-size',
  3827	   314	    ok: journalBytes <= JOURNAL_WARN_BYTES,
  3828	   315	    detail: journalBytes > JOURNAL_WARN_BYTES ? `journal is ${(journalBytes / 1048576).toFixed(1)} MB (> 5 MB) — finalize to rotate it` : `${journalBytes} bytes`,
  3829	   316	  });
  3830	   317	
  3831	   318	
  3832	   319	  // Platform probes + version probes + mount detection: every non-git child
  3833	   320	  // invocation rides the same 15-min cache in the state dir.
  3834	   321	  const cachePath = `${p.logDir}/probe-cache.json`;
  3835	   322	  const cached = safeReadJson(io.fs, cachePath);
  3836	   323	  /** @type {any[]} */
  3837	   324	  let platforms;
  3838	   325	  /** @type {Record<string, string | null>} */
  3839	   326	  let versions;
  3840	   327	  /** @type {string | null} */
  3841	   328	  let mountSource;
  3842	   329	  if (cached.ok && typeof cached.value?.at === 'string' && Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(cached.value.at) < PROBE_CACHE_MS && Array.isArray(cached.value.records)) {
  3843	   330	    platforms = cached.value.records;
  3844	   331	    versions = cached.value.versions ?? {};
  3845	   332	    mountSource = cached.value.mount ?? null;
  3846	   333	  } else {
  3847	   334	    /** @type {any} */
  3848	   335	    let table = null;
  3849	   336	    try {
  3850	   337	      // Packaged data, not repo state: read through the real fs so probes can
  3851	   338	      // classify limit responses regardless of the injected io.fs contents.
  3852	   339	      table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, { fs: realFs });
  3853	   340	    } catch {
  3854	   341	      table = null;
  3855	   342	    }
  3856	   343	    platforms = [];
  3857	   344	    for (const probe of PROBES) platforms.push(await runProbe(probe, io, table));
  3858	   345	    versions = {};
  3859	   346	    for (const platform of Object.keys(VERSION_BINS)) versions[platform] = await versionOf(platform, io);
  3860	   347	    mountSource = null;
  3861	   348	    try {
  3862	   349	      const df = await io.execFile('df', ['-P', root], { timeout: 2000 });
  3863	   350	      mountSource = ((df.stdout ?? '').split('\n')[1] ?? '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || null;
  3864	   351	    } catch {
  3865	   352	      mountSource = null;
  3866	   353	    }
  3867	   354	    // Jail before writing the probe cache (iter-3 F4): the cache rides
  3868	   355	    // .handoff/log, so a symlinked .handoff or log dir must never redirect the
  3869	   356	    // write outside the repository (threat model). Diagnostics are best-effort —
  3870	   357	    // on an unsafe tree we simply skip caching; the atomic write already
  3871	   358	    // prevents a torn cache under concurrent doctors.
  3872	   359	    if (checkHandoffTree(root, io).ok) {
  3873	   360	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.logDir);

  4953	     1	# Review artifacts
  4954	     2	
  4955	     3	Every review gate leaves an auditable trail here. Layout:
  4956	     4	
  4957	     5	```
  4958	     6	reviews/
  4959	     7	  _templates/               # prompt templates per gate type
  4960	     8	  <task-id>/                # YYYY-MM-DD-slug
  4961	     9	    gate-1-plan/
  4962	    10	      iteration-NN/{prompt.md, verdict.md}
  4963	    11	    subtask-<name>-tests/
  4964	    12	      iteration-NN/{prompt.md, verdict.md}
  4965	    13	    gate-2-final/
  4966	    14	      reviewer-a/iteration-NN/{prompt.md, verdict.md}
  4967	    15	      reviewer-b/iteration-NN/{prompt.md, verdict.md}
  4968	    16	      findings.md           # collated blocking / non-blocking items
  4969	    17	```
  4970	    18	
  4971	    19	Verdicts: `APPROVED` / `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES` / `BLOCKED`. Notes are folded in; BLOCKED means revise and re-review.
  4972	    20	
  4973	    21	Every `verdict.md` starts with a header block:
  4974	    22	
  4975	    23	```
  4976	    24	role: <plan-reviewer | test-verifier | final-reviewer-a | final-reviewer-b>
  4977	    25	model: <concrete model @ effort actually used>
  4978	    26	harness: <how it was invoked>
  4979	    27	date: <YYYY-MM-DD>
  4980	    28	verdict: <APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED>
  4981	    29	degraded: <none | single-vendor | model-fallback | ...>
  4982	    30	```
  4983	    31	
  4984	    32	Iteration budget: a gate loops at most **5** times. After the 5th iteration the gate closes; outstanding findings escalate to Saksham for a decision.
  4985	
  4986	 succeeded in 0ms:
  4987	     1	# Core engine design — module APIs and test lists
  4988	     2	
  4989	     3	Authoritative plan: `docs/plans/2026-07-11-baton-v1.md` (Gate-1 approved). Where this doc and the plan differ, **the plan wins** — the Concurrency, Receive-transaction, Journal-rotation, Lock-model, and Detector sections were amended across Gate-1 iterations 1–5 and are specified there in full.
  4990	     4	
  4991	     5	## Language and tooling
  4992	     6	
  4993	     7	Plain ESM JavaScript (`.mjs`) with JSDoc types, checked by `tsc --noEmit --checkJs` (dev-only). No build step: hooks invoke source directly. Zero runtime dependencies; strict spec-based flag parsing (`parseFlagsStrict` in commands/shared.mjs — unknown flags, stray positionals, and missing string-flag values are exit-2 usage errors, so a garbled safety flag can never silently lose intent); `node:test` with a small golden-file helper (`UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 npm test` regenerates). Node >= 20.
  4994	     8	
  4995	     9	Testability backbone: every command's `run()` takes an injected `io` object — `{ cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }`. Pure logic (reducers, renderers, resolvers, classifiers) takes plain data and returns plain data. Unit tests run over an in-memory fs fake (`tests/helpers/memfs.mjs`); a thin integration layer uses `fs.mkdtemp` on the real fs.
  4996	    10	
  4997	    11	## File tree
  4998	    12	
  4999	    13	```
  5000	    14	core/
  5001	    15	├── bin/baton.mjs             # shebang; delegates to src/cli.mjs
  5002	    16	├── src/
  5003	    17	│   ├── cli.mjs               # routing, --json envelope, exit-code contract
  5004	    18	│   ├── commands/             # checkpoint, finalize, detect, remap, receive,
  5005	    19	│   │                         #   init, doctor, status, purge-transcript, recover
  5006	    20	│   ├── bundle/
  5007	    21	│   │   ├── schema.mjs        # emptyBundle, validateBundle, migrate
  5008	    22	│   │   ├── store.mjs         # load/write .handoff/, journal, recovery, rotation
  5009	    23	│   │   ├── lock.mjs          # operation-scoped lock (see plan §Lock model)
  5010	    24	│   │   ├── merge.mjs         # applyEvent reducer (pure)
  5011	    25	│   │   ├── normalize.mjs     # hook payload -> baton/event@1 extractors
  5012	    26	│   │   ├── compact.mjs       # deterministic size-budget enforcement (pure)
  5013	    27	│   │   └── render.mjs        # bundle -> HANDOFF.md (pure)
  5014	    28	│   ├── detect/
  5015	    29	│   │   ├── signatures.mjs    # load + validate + overlay signature tables
  5016	    30	│   │   └── classifier.mjs    # classify(text, exitCode, platform) (pure)
  5017	    31	│   ├── roles/
  5018	    32	│   │   ├── matrix.mjs        # load/validate baton.config.json
  5019	    33	│   │   └── resolve.mjs       # remap resolver (pure, explainable)
  5020	    34	│   ├── receive/
  5021	    35	│   │   ├── prompt.mjs        # resume-prompt renderer (pure, <=2500 chars)
  5022	    36	│   │   └── txn.mjs           # prepare/commit transaction + receipt tokens

  5024	    38	│   │   ├── plan.mjs          # planInit -> Action[]; applyActions
  5025	    39	│   │   ├── gitignore.mjs     # ensure-line (pure text transform)
  5026	    40	│   │   ├── managed-block.mjs # <!-- baton:begin/end --> merges
  5027	    41	│   │   └── attribution.mjs   # .claude/settings.json deep-merge (pure)
  5028	    42	│   ├── git/
  5029	    43	│   │   ├── snapshot.mjs      # branch/HEAD/dirty + content digest via execFile
  5030	    44	│   │   └── guard.mjs         # scan recent commits for AI trailers
  5031	    45	│   └── util/
  5032	    46	│       ├── fsx.mjs           # atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, backupThenWrite
  5033	    47	│       ├── jsonl.mjs         # appendEntry, readAllTolerant
  5034	    48	│       └── ids.mjs           # bundleId, event dedupeKey (sha256 canonical)
  5035	    49	└── data/signatures.v1.json   # versioned limit-signature table (data, not code)
  5036	    50	```
  5037	    51	
  5038	    52	Tests mirror this: `tests/{helpers,unit,commands,invariants,integration}/`.
  5039	    53	
  5040	    54	## Bundle schema (`baton/bundle@1`)
  5041	    55	
  5042	    56	`bundle.json` fields: `schema`, `bundleId`, `generation`, `createdAt/updatedAt`, `origin {platform, model, sessionHint, unstable}`, `task {goal, constraints[], acceptance[]}`, `plan.steps[] {id, title, status: pending|active|done|blocked, note}`, `decisions[] {seq, ts, summary, detail(capped)}`, `files {touched[] {path, op, lastTs}, truncated}`, `roles.assignments`, `git {branch, headSha, dirty, dirtySummary[], contentDigest, summaryTruncated} | null`, `handoff {status: open|sealed|received, reason, reasonClass, toPlatformHint, finalizedAt, receive_log[]}`, `journalSeq`, `compaction {droppedDecisions, note}`, `dedupeRing: string[]` (merge idempotency ring, capped 500, oldest evicted), transcript field (opt-in, redacted).
  5043	    57	
  5044	    58	`journal.ndjson` entry: `{seq, ts, type, dedupeKey, writerId, source, payload}`. Types: `decision | plan.set | plan.step | file.touch | note | roles.remap | task.update | git.update`. Every type is self-applicable for replay over `emptyBundle()`.
  5045	    59	
  5046	    60	Size budget (pure `compact.mjs`): decisions capped first-20 + last-150 + marker; `detail` <= 2000 chars; `files.touched` <= 300 (LRU by `lastTs`, sets `files.truncated: true`); `git.dirtySummary` <= 100 lines (sets `git.summaryTruncated: true`; skipped when `git` is null). Deterministic truncation only — no LLM summarization.
  5047	    61	
  5048	    62	## Module APIs
  5049	    63	
  5050	    64	```js
  5051	    65	// bundle/schema.mjs
  5052	    66	emptyBundle({platform, model, goal}, nowIso) -> Bundle
  5053	    67	validateBundle(obj) -> {ok, errors: [{path, msg}]}    // hand-rolled, no deps
  5054	    68	migrate(obj) -> Bundle                                 // identity in v1
  5055	    69	
  5056	    70	// bundle/store.mjs  (all take io)
  5057	    71	bundlePaths(root) -> {dir, snapshot, bak, journal, handoffMd, historyDir, lockDir, logDir}
  5058	    72	loadBundle(root, io) -> {bundle, warnings[]}           // snapshot + replay + recovery
  5059	    73	writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) -> void                // bak -> tmp -> rename, under lock
  5060	    74	appendJournal(root, entry, io) -> seq                  // seq allocated under lock
  5061	    75	rotateJournal(root, kind, io) -> historyPath           // marker-based, crash-safe (plan §rotation)
  5062	    76	
  5063	    77	// bundle/lock.mjs — operation-scoped; see plan §Lock model for takeover rules
  5064	    78	withLock(root, io, fn) -> result                       // acquire -> fn -> release
  5065	    79	recoverLock(root, io, {force}) -> {recovered, refusedReason}
  5066	    80	
  5067	    81	// bundle/merge.mjs (pure)
  5068	    82	applyEvent(bundle, event) -> Bundle                    // idempotent via dedupeKey ring (500)
  5069	    83	
  5070	    84	// bundle/normalize.mjs
  5071	    85	normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform) -> Event[]         // never throws; unknowns -> note
  5072	    86	
  5073	    87	// bundle/render.mjs (pure)
  5074	    88	renderHandoffMd(bundle) -> string                      // golden-tested, no AI-credit strings
  5075	    89	
  5076	    90	// detect/
  5077	    91	loadSignatures({builtinPath, overlayPath}, io) -> Table // validates, lints regexes
  5078	    92	classify({text, exitCode, platform, table, structured}) ->
  5079	    93	  {class, signatureId, confidence, resetHint, tried?}   // exit-code rule: plan §detector
  5080	    94	
  5081	    95	// roles/
  5082	    96	loadConfig(cwd, io) -> {config, errors[]}
  5083	    97	resolveRoles({config, to, avoid, nativeOnly, probes}) ->
  5084	    98	  {assignments: {[role]: {platform, model, mode, chainIndex, skipped[]}}, notes[]}
  5085	    99	
  5086	   100	// receive/txn.mjs — see plan §Concurrency for full transition semantics
  5087	   101	prepare(root, {platform, origin, reason}, io) -> {token, prompt, assignments, warnings[]}
  5088	   102	commit(root, token, io) -> {adopted, generation, archivedTo}   // rejects bound-input drift
  5089	   103	
  5090	   104	// scaffold/
  5091	   105	planInit(cwd, opts, io) -> Action[]                    // {path, kind, reason, preview}
  5092	   106	applyActions(actions, io) -> Applied[]
  5093	   107	mergeAttribution(existingJsonText|null, {force}) -> {text, changed, warnings} | {error}
  5094	   108	
  5095	   109	// git/
  5096	   110	snapshot({execFile, cwd}) -> {branch, headSha, dirty, dirtySummary, contentDigest} // 5s timeout
  5097	   111	scanCommitsForAiTrailers({execFile, cwd, limit: 50}) -> [{sha, line, pattern}]
  5098	   112	```
  5099	   113	
  5100	   114	## Per-module test lists
  5101	   115	
  5102	   116	- **fsx**: atomic write visible-only-after-rename; `.bak` rotation; leftover `.tmp` ignored/cleaned; `safeReadJson` on garbage; Windows rename-retry (EPERM).
  5103	   117	- **jsonl/ids**: append+read roundtrip; torn last line dropped with warning; seq monotonic; dedupeKey stable across key order.
  5104	   118	- **schema**: empty bundle validates; pathed error per missing field; unknown fields tolerated; `@2` rejected by migrate.
  5105	   119	- **merge**: each event type mutates its section; double-apply = identical bundle; unknown type degrades to note; seq ordering.
  5106	   120	- **compact**: over-budget collapse deterministic (byte-identical); under-budget identity; truncation markers.
  5107	   121	- **store**: snapshot+replay equals pure reduction; corrupt snapshot -> `.bak` recovery; both corrupt -> journal rebuild; torn tail; marker-based rotation roll-forward/back; kill-mid-write sim.
  5108	   122	- **lock**: mutual exclusion; provably-dead takeover; live same-host owner refused (incl. `--force`); torn metadata refused; pause-after-final-fence-check; crash between creation and metadata publication.
  5109	   123	- **normalize**: `baton/event@1` passthrough; per-harness fixture extraction (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor payloads); malformed-input fuzz never throws.
  5110	   124	- **render / receive-prompt**: goldens (minimal, mid-session, finalized, stale, degraded-role); char caps; attribution invariant; determinism.
  5111	   125	- **signatures/classifier**: verbatim positive fixtures per platform; negative/near-miss corpus; ANSI stripping; precedence; reset-hint extraction; exit-code interplay matrix; non-limit failing-process fixtures; overlay override; bad-regex rejection; time guard.
  5112	   126	- **matrix/resolve**: config validation (pathed errors); chain walking with avoid/disabled/rate-limited skips; native/delegated/forced-default/unavailable; probe-eligibility rules; determinism.
  5113	   127	- **txn**: token binds journalSeq + intake + config digest + session + probe snapshot + content-sensitive git digest; one rejection test per bound input; `sealed->received` and `open->received` (degraded seal); ownership adoption + new generation; chained A->B->C; competing receivers; double-commit.
  5114	   128	- **scaffold**: gitignore variants (covering pattern no-op, CRLF, trailing newline, idempotent double-run); managed-block replace/append/corrupt-marker-refuse with outside-bytes invariance; attribution merge (absent/present/non-empty/malformed-refuse); `--dry-run` zero writes; real-fs `mkdtemp` + `git init` integration.
  5115	   129	- **git**: snapshot on clean/dirty/detached/not-a-repo/timeout; guard trailer fixtures incl. word-boundary false-positive ("Claudette Smith" not flagged).
  5116	   130	- **doctor**: check aggregation; `--strict` exit; probe states; version floors; network-FS warning.
  5117	   131	- **purge-transcript**: whole-tree secret removal (snapshot, bak, journal, history, log); interruption resume via marker; packaged-bin invocation.
  5118	   132	- **invariants**: no AI-credit strings in any rendered output, golden, or template.
  5119	   133	- **integration**: e2e failover (init -> 25 checkpoints -> kill-mid-write -> finalize usage-limit -> receive on second platform -> golden prompt); chained A->B->C; six-direction contract matrix; cli-spawn exit codes and `--json` stdout purity.
  5120	   134	
  5121	   135	## CLI contract
  5122	   136	
  5123	   137	Commands: `checkpoint`, `finalize`, `detect`, `remap`, `receive`, `init`, `doctor`, `status`, `purge-transcript`, `recover`, `session-start`. Exit codes: `0` ok · `1` command failure · `2` usage error · `10` usage-limit · `11` throttle · `12` auth · `13` other-error. `--json`: exactly one `{ok, data, warnings, error}` envelope on stdout, diagnostics on stderr. Checkpoint soft-fails to exit 0 in hook contexts (`--strict` opts out). `session-start` is the cheap adapter SessionStart gate: soft paths always exit 0 and its BARE stdout is harness-shaped context (not an envelope); with `--json` it honors the envelope contract (`data: {pending, shaped}`). `wrap` is reserved, not implemented in v1.

  5138	    11	## Claude Code
  5139	    12	
  5140	    13	- Hook events include `Stop`, `StopFailure`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `SessionStart`, `SessionEnd`, `PostToolUseFailure`, and others.
  5141	    14	- **`StopFailure`** fires when a turn ends due to an API error; output and exit code are ignored (observability-only). Matcher filters by error type. **Enum snapshot (2026-07-11, code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks):** `rate_limit`, `overloaded`, `authentication_failed`, `oauth_org_not_allowed`, `billing_error`, `invalid_request`, `model_not_found`, `server_error`, `max_output_tokens`, `unknown`. Note: a Gate-1 reviewer disputed `overloaded`'s presence in the current enum — fixtures are generated from this snapshot and unknown values normalize to `unknown`, so drift is safe either way. Re-snapshot before freezing fixtures.
  5142	    15	- Limit strings (fallback tier): `You've hit your session limit · resets …` / `weekly limit` / `Opus limit`; throttle: `Server is temporarily limiting requests (not your usage limit)`; `API Error: Request rejected (429)`.
  5143	    16	- **Live capture (2026-07-12, this machine)**: a subagent died with `Agent terminated early due to an API error: You've hit your session limit · resets 2am (Asia/Dubai)` — reset hints can carry a timezone suffix in parentheses; the resetHint extractor and fixtures must cover this variant.
  5144	    17	- Attribution: `"attribution": {"commit": "", "pr": ""}` in settings.json (`includeCoAuthoredBy` is deprecated).
  5145	    18	
  5146	    19	## Codex CLI (>= 0.144; this machine: 0.144.1)
  5147	    20	
  5148	    21	- Lifecycle hooks: `SessionStart`, `SubagentStart`, `PreToolUse`, `PermissionRequest`, `PostToolUse`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `SubagentStop`, `Stop`. Config: `~/.codex/hooks.json`, `<repo>/.codex/hooks.json`, or config.toml `[hooks]` tables. Payload: stdin JSON with `session_id`, `cwd`, `hook_event_name`, `model`, `transcript_path` (+ `turn_id` for turn-scoped).
  5149	    22	- **Trust gate**: non-managed command hooks require review of the exact definition; trust recorded against the hook's hash; new/changed hooks skipped until re-trusted via `/hooks`. Project hooks load only when the project `.codex/` layer is trusted.
  5150	    23	- `commandWindows` / `command_windows` = Windows-only command override.
  5151	    24	- Custom prompts (`~/.codex/prompts/*.md`) are deprecated upstream — legacy fallback only.
  5152	    25	- Skills: Agent Skills standard at `.agents/skills` (repo) and `~/.agents/skills` (user).
  5153	    26	- Limit string: `You've hit your usage limit. … try again at [date/time]`; `codex exec --json` exits non-zero on failure (nonzero alone never classifies as usage-limit).
  5154	    27	- gpt-5.6-sol is server-gated by CLI version (rejected on 0.135.0 with "requires a newer version of Codex"; works on 0.144.1).
  5155	    28	
  5156	    29	## Cursor
  5157	    30	
  5158	    31	- Hook events (verified; cursor.com/docs/agent/hooks): `sessionStart`, `sessionEnd`, `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`, `postToolUseFailure`, `subagentStart`, `subagentStop`, `beforeShellExecution`, `afterShellExecution`, `beforeMCPExecution`, `afterMCPExecution`, `beforeReadFile`, `afterFileEdit`, `beforeTabFileRead`, `afterTabFileEdit`, `beforeSubmitPrompt`, `preCompact`, `afterAgentResponse`, `afterAgentThought`, `stop`, `workspaceOpen`.
  5159	    32	- **There is no pre-commit/VCS hook.** Agent-issued commits are observable via `beforeShellExecution` with a git-commit matcher; SCM-UI commits are invisible.
  5160	    33	- Config: `.cursor/hooks.json` (project; requires trusted workspace), `~/.cursor/hooks.json` (user), enterprise/MDM paths. Format: `{"version": 1, "hooks": {"<event>": [{"command", "timeout", "matcher"}]}}`.
  5161	    34	- No `commandWindows` variant — cross-platform command strings.
  5162	    35	- BYO-key rate-limit JSON: `{"error": "Too Many Requests", "message": "Rate limit exceeded…"}`; managed-billing error schema undocumented (heuristics, `confidence: low`).
  5163	    36	- cursor-agent reads `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md` natively; `.cursor/commands` CLI loading unconfirmed — `baton receive --print-prompt` is the universal fallback.

  5594	 succeeded in 0ms:
  5595	     1	/**
  5596	     2	 * Ensure an ignore pattern is active in a .gitignore body. Pure text
  5597	     3	 * transform: a covering non-comment line ('.handoff/' or '.handoff') makes
  5598	     4	 * this a byte-for-byte no-op; otherwise the pattern is appended on its own
  5599	     5	 * line with a single trailing newline.
  5600	     6	 * @param {string | null} existingText current .gitignore body, or null when absent
  5601	     7	 * @param {string} pattern the ignore entry to guarantee (e.g. '.handoff/')
  5602	     8	 * @returns {{text: string, changed: boolean}}
  5603	     9	 */
  5604	    10	export function ensureIgnoreLine(existingText, pattern) {
  5605	    11	  const body = existingText ?? '';
  5606	    12	  const bare = pattern.endsWith('/') ? pattern.slice(0, -1) : pattern;
  5607	    13	  const covers = body
  5608	    14	    .split(/\r?\n/)
  5609	    15	    .map((l) => l.trim())
  5610	    16	    .some((l) => l === pattern || l === bare);
  5611	    17	  if (covers) return { text: body, changed: false };
  5612	    18	
  5613	    19	  if (body === '') return { text: pattern + '\n', changed: true };
  5614	    20	  const joined = body.endsWith('\n') ? body : body + '\n';
  5615	    21	  return { text: joined + pattern + '\n', changed: true };
  5616	    22	}

  5626	     6	
  5627	     7	/**
  5628	     8	 * `baton init` — two-phase scaffolder. The plan phase (planInit) is read-only;
  5629	     9	 * `--dry-run` stops there. Apply writes each planned action atomically;
  5630	    10	 * refusals (malformed settings, corrupt markers) warn and leave their file
  5631	    11	 * untouched while the rest of the scaffold still lands.
  5632	    12	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
  5633	    13	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  5634	    14	 */
  5635	    15	export async function cmdInit(args, io) {
  5636	    16	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { codex: 'boolean', cursor: 'boolean', 'with-legacy-prompts': 'boolean', force: 'boolean', check: 'boolean', 'dry-run': 'boolean' });
  5637	    17	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'init', parsed.error);
  5638	    18	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  5639	    19	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  5640	    20	
  5641	    21	  // The strict parser rejects stray tokens outright (a pasted `# comment` used
  5642	    22	  // to be consumed as --dry-run's "value", silently running a REAL init), so
  5643	    23	  // boolean flags here are only ever true | undefined.
  5644	    24	  const checkMode = flags.check === true;
  5645	    25	  const dryRun = flags['dry-run'] === true;
  5646	    26	
  5647	    27	  /** @type {ReturnType<typeof planInit>} */
  5648	    28	  let actions;
  5649	    29	  try {
  5650	    30	    const base = planInit(root, { force: flags.force === true }, io);
  5651	    31	    // Feed the harness writer the gitignore text AS THE BASE SCAFFOLD LEAVES IT
  5652	    32	    // (planned write, else current file) so its own ignore lines chain on top —
  5653	    33	    // two independent reads would make the later write clobber the earlier one.
  5654	    34	    const gi = base.find((a) => a.id === 'gitignore');
  5655	    35	    /** @type {string | null} */
  5656	    36	    let gitignoreBase = null;
  5657	    37	    if (gi?.op === 'write') {
  5658	    38	      gitignoreBase = /** @type {string} */ (gi.preview);
  5659	    39	    } else {
  5660	    40	      try {
  5661	    41	        gitignoreBase = io.fs.readFileSync(`${root}/.gitignore`, 'utf8');
  5662	    42	      } catch {
  5663	    43	        gitignoreBase = null;
  5664	    44	      }
  5665	    45	    }
  5666	    46	    actions = [
  5667	    47	      ...base,
  5668	    48	      ...planHarnessInit(
  5669	    49	        root,
  5670	    50	        { codex: flags.codex === true, cursor: flags.cursor === true, withLegacyPrompts: flags['with-legacy-prompts'] === true, gitignoreBase },
  5671	    51	        io,
  5672	    52	      ),
  5673	    53	    ];
  5674	    54	  } catch (err) {
  5675	    55	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
  5676	    56	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'plan-failed', msg } });
  5677	    57	    else io.stderr.write(`baton init: ${msg}\n`);
  5678	    58	    return 1;
  5679	    59	  }
  5680	    60	
  5681	    61	  const describe = (/** @type {any} */ a) =>
  5682	    62	    a.op === 'write' ? `write ${a.path}` : a.op === 'skip' ? `skip  ${a.path} (${a.note})` : `REFUSE ${a.path} — ${a.note}`;
  5683	    63	
  5684	    64	  // --check (gate-2 fix 9): the CI drift gate. Read-only — a scaffolded tree
  5685	    65	  // where init would change nothing passes; anything init would write or
  5686	    66	  // refuse is drift and fails the check.
  5687	    67	  if (checkMode) {
  5688	    68	    const drift = actions.filter((a) => a.op !== 'skip');
  5689	    69	    if (flags.json) {
  5690	    70	      emitEnvelope(io, {
  5691	    71	        ok: drift.length === 0,
  5692	    72	        data: { drift: drift.map(({ id, path, op, note }) => ({ id, path, op, note: note ?? null })) },
  5693	    73	      });
  5694	    74	    } else if (drift.length === 0) {
  5695	    75	      io.stdout.write('baton init --check: no drift — rendered files match template output\n');
  5696	    76	    } else {
  5697	    77	      io.stderr.write(`baton init --check: drift detected — init would write:\n${drift.map((a) => `  ${describe(a)}`).join('\n')}\n`);
  5698	    78	    }
  5699	    79	    return drift.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
  5700	    80	  }
  5701	    81	
  5702	    82	  if (dryRun) {
  5703	    83	    if (flags.json) {
  5704	    84	      emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { dryRun: true, actions: actions.map(({ id, path, op, note }) => ({ id, path, op, note: note ?? null })) } });
  5705	    85	    } else {
  5706	    86	      io.stdout.write(`baton init plan (dry run — nothing written):\n${actions.map((a) => `  ${describe(a)}`).join('\n')}\n`);
  5707	    87	    }
  5708	    88	    return 0;
  5709	    89	  }
  5710	    90	
  5711	    91	  /** @type {string[]} */
  5712	    92	  const warnings = [];
  5713	    93	  for (const a of actions) {
  5714	    94	    if (a.op === 'refuse') {
  5715	    95	      warnings.push(a.note ?? `refused ${a.path}`);
  5716	    96	      continue;
  5717	    97	    }
  5718	    98	    if (a.op !== 'write') continue;
  5719	    99	    ensureDir(io.fs, dirname(a.path));
  5720	   100	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, a.path, /** @type {string} */ (a.preview));
  5721	   101	  }
  5722	   102	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton init: ${w} — left untouched\n`);
  5723	   103	
  5724	   104	  if (flags.json) {
  5725	   105	    emitEnvelope(io, {
  5726	   106	      ok: true,
  5727	   107	      data: { actions: actions.map(({ id, path, op, note }) => ({ id, path, op, note: note ?? null })) },
  5728	   108	      warnings,
  5729	   109	    });
  5730	   110	  } else {
  5731	   111	    io.stdout.write(`baton init:\n${actions.map((a) => `  ${describe(a)}`).join('\n')}\n`);
  5732	   112	  }
  5733	   113	  return 0;
  5734	   114	}

  5774	    36	//       later is scanned automatically) and scanned; the arm fails if the
  5775	    37	//       directory yields no files.
  5776	    38	//   A3. LIVE RENDERED OUTPUT. renderHandoffMd + renderResumePrompt over
  5777	    39	//       canonical inputs.
  5778	    40	//   A4. SCAFFOLD PREVIEWS. planInit(cwd, opts, io) -> Action[] with
  5779	    41	//       Action.preview: string (the exact bytes init would write), called over
  5780	    42	//       an empty real-fs tmp cwd — the rendered-scaffold arm; at least one
  5781	    43	//       preview must be scanned.
  5782	    44	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  5783	    45	
  5784	    46	// Mirrors tests/unit/receive-prompt.test.mjs (and render.test.mjs) VERBATIM.
  5785	    47	// Deliberately narrow so it never trips on legitimate platform/model identifiers
  5786	    48	// ("claude-code", "gpt-5.6-sol") that are real data, not attribution.
  5787	    49	const ATTRIBUTION_PATTERNS = [
  5788	    50	  /co-authored-by/i,
  5789	    51	  /generated with claude/i,
  5790	    52	  /generated by claude/i,
  5791	    53	  /🤖/,
  5792	    54	  /written by (an? )?ai\b/i,
  5793	    55	  /authored by (an? )?ai\b/i,
  5794	    56	  /powered by claude/i,
  5795	    57	];
  5796	    58	const scanAttribution = (text) => ATTRIBUTION_PATTERNS.filter((re) => re.test(text)).map((re) => re.source);
  5797	    59	
  5798	    60	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  5799	    61	const repoRoot = join(here, '..', '..');
  5800	    62	const TEMPLATES_DIR = join(repoRoot, 'templates');
  5801	    63	const GOLDEN_DIR = join(repoRoot, 'tests', 'helpers', 'fixtures', 'golden');
  5802	    64	
  5803	    65	/** Recursively collect every file path under `dir` on the real fs. */
  5804	    66	function walkFiles(dir) {
  5805	    67	  /** @type {string[]} */
  5806	    68	  const out = [];
  5807	    69	  if (!existsSync(dir)) return out;
  5808	    70	  for (const name of readdirSync(dir)) {
  5809	    71	    const p = join(dir, name);
  5810	    72	    if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) out.push(...walkFiles(p));
  5811	    73	    else out.push(p);
  5812	    74	  }
  5813	    75	  return out;
  5814	    76	}
  5815	    77	
  5816	    78	const T0 = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z';
  5817	    79	
  5818	    80	function liveBundle() {
  5819	    81	  return {
  5820	    82	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
  5821	    83	    bundleId: 'b_inv0000000000',
  5822	    84	    generation: 1,
  5823	    85	    createdAt: T0,
  5824	    86	    updatedAt: T0,
  5825	    87	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: null, unstable: false },
  5826	    88	    task: { goal: 'Scan every rendered output', constraints: ['Deterministic output'], acceptance: [] },
  5827	    89	    plan: { steps: [{ id: 's1', title: 'Render', status: 'active', note: null }] },
  5828	    90	    decisions: [{ seq: 1, ts: T0, summary: 'no attribution anywhere' }],
  5829	    91	    files: { touched: [{ path: 'README.md', op: 'edit', lastTs: T0 }] },
  5830	    92	    roles: { assignments: {} },
  5831	    93	    git: null,
  5832	    94	    handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
  5833	    95	    journalSeq: 1,
  5834	    96	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
  5835	    97	    dedupeRing: [],
  5836	    98	  };
  5837	    99	}
  5838	   100	
  5839	   101	function liveResumeInputs() {
  5840	   102	  return {
  5841	   103	    bundle: liveBundle(),
  5842	   104	    assignments: { implementer: { platform: 'codex', model: 'gpt-5.6-sol', mode: 'native' } },
  5843	   105	    warnings: [],
  5844	   106	    origin: 'claude-code',
  5845	   107	    reason: "You've hit your usage limit",
  5846	   108	  };
  5847	   109	}

  5891	   153	describe('attribution invariant — scaffold previews (A4, planInit)', () => {
  5892	   154	  it('every preview baton init would write carries no AI-credit string', () => {
  5893	   155	    const cwd = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'baton-attr-inv-'));
  5894	   156	    try {
  5895	   157	      const io = {
  5896	   158	        cwd,
  5897	   159	        env: {},
  5898	   160	        stdin: '',
  5899	   161	        stdout: { write: () => true },
  5900	   162	        stderr: { write: () => true },
  5901	   163	        fs: nodeFs,
  5902	   164	        execFile: () => Promise.reject(new Error('no exec in invariant scan')),
  5903	   165	        now: () => T0,
  5904	   166	        host: 'inv-host',
  5905	   167	        pid: 4242,
  5906	   168	        startTime: 111000,
  5907	   169	        processAlive: (pid) => pid === 4242,
  5908	   170	        newFencingToken: () => 'inv-tok',
  5909	   171	      };
  5910	   172	      const actions = planInit(cwd, {}, io);
  5911	   173	      assert.ok(Array.isArray(actions) && actions.length > 0, 'planInit yields scaffold actions to scan');
  5912	   174	
  5913	   175	      let previewsScanned = 0;

  5918	   180	      }
  5919	   181	      assert.ok(previewsScanned > 0, 'at least one scaffold preview was scanned (the rendered-scaffold arm is non-vacuous)');
  5920	   182	    } finally {
  5921	   183	      rmSync(cwd, { recursive: true, force: true });
  5922	   184	    }
  5923	   185	  });

  6044	     9	
  6045	    10	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  6046	    11	// Contract choices for core/src/receive/txn.mjs (docs/design/core.md §Module APIs
  6047	    12	// prepare/commit; §Per-module test lists "txn"; plan §Concurrency — the receive
  6048	    13	// prepare/commit two-phase transaction, receipt-token bindings incl. the
  6049	    14	// content-sensitive git digest, open->received degraded seal, ownership adoption +
  6050	    15	// new generation, chained failover, competing receivers, double-commit).
  6051	    16	//
  6052	    17	// SIGNATURES (pinned — the task overrides core.md's 3-arg commit):
  6053	    18	//   prepare(root, opts, io) -> { token, prompt, assignments, warnings }
  6054	    19	//   commit(root, token, opts, io) -> { adopted, generation, archivedTo }
  6055	    20	//   Both are SYNCHRONOUS. The plan pins that the receive COMMAND wires
  6056	    21	//   core/src/git/snapshot.mjs in and passes the result as opts.gitSnapshot; txn
  6057	    22	//   itself never spawns git — it takes the snapshot as plain data.
  6058	    23	//
  6059	    24	//   opts (same shape for prepare and commit):
  6060	    25	//     { platform,       // destination harness we are resuming ON (e.g. 'codex')
  6061	    26	//       origin,         // intake: the harness handed off FROM (e.g. 'claude-code')
  6062	    27	//       reason,         // intake: why the switch happened (free text)
  6063	    28	//       gitSnapshot,    // the caller-computed git snapshot object (git/snapshot.mjs)
  6064	    29	//       probes,         // optional probe map for resolveRoles (default null)
  6065	    30	//       sessionHint }   // the RECEIVING session's hint, used for ownership adoption
  6066	    31	//
  6067	    32	// TOKEN BINDING (THE pin). prepare captures a REVISION FINGERPRINT and returns it
  6068	    33	//   as an opaque token. It binds every one of these inputs (one rejection test per
  6069	    34	//   input below — verifier fold F3 completed the set):
  6070	    35	//     - bundle REVISION: generation + journalSeq (as loadBundle reports them)
  6071	    36	//     - intake: origin + reason
  6072	    37	//     - config digest: a hash of the baton.config.json bytes at <root>
  6073	    38	//     - GIT digest: a hash of the WHOLE opts.gitSnapshot object (PIN — the git
  6074	    39	//       state is bound by hashing the caller-passed object, so headSha, dirty,
  6075	    40	//       AND contentDigest are each binding: a HEAD move with an UNCHANGED
  6076	    41	//       contentDigest still drifts the token, and vice versa)
  6077	    42	//     - probe snapshot: a hash of opts.probes
  6078	    43	//     - target platform (opts.platform)
  6079	    44	//     - receiving session (opts.sessionHint) — plan: the token binds "target
  6080	    45	//       platform+session"
  6081	    46	//   prepare mutates NOTHING (it cannot persist a "prepared" record — the token is
  6082	    47	//   self-contained). commit RE-DERIVES every bound input from current disk state +
  6083	    48	//   its own opts and compares to the token; ANY drift throws an Error whose message
  6084	    49	//   names re-prepare (matches /re-?prepare/i) and performs NO mutation (the
  6085	    50	//   .handoff tree is byte-identical before and after the rejected commit). Because
  6086	    51	//   a successful commit bumps `generation`, an already-spent token (double-commit)
  6087	    52	//   and a losing competitor's token (competing receivers) both fail the revision
  6088	    53	//   check with no extra bookkeeping.
  6089	    54	//
  6090	    55	// COMMIT SUCCESS (one atomic transition):
  6091	    56	//   - Archives the current bundle to history/ via rotateJournal kind 'receive'
  6092	    57	//     (a history/<stem>.receive.json + .ndjson pair). archivedTo === that stem.
  6093	    58	//     The archived .receive freeze is the RECEIVED seal (handoff.status
  6094	    59	//     'received' — asserted on BOTH the sealed and open paths, verifier fold F7:
  6095	    60	//     archiving the pre-commit sealed bundle unchanged would be wrong); it
  6096	    61	//     retains the receipt data (the receive_log entry for THIS receive rides in
  6097	    62	//     the archived freeze). For an OPEN source bundle it is the receive-generated
  6098	    63	//     DEGRADED seal (handoff.reason from intake, reasonClass classified from the
  6099	    64	//     reason text against the intake ORIGIN platform via the built-in signature
  6100	    65	//     table); for a SEALED source the finalize reason/reasonClass are retained.
  6101	    66	//   - Adopts ownership: origin becomes the RECEIVING side — origin.platform ===
  6102	    67	//     opts.platform, origin.sessionHint === opts.sessionHint.
  6103	    68	//   - Opens a fresh writable generation: generation + 1, handoff.status 'open'.
  6104	    69	//   - Appends a receive_log entry { origin, reason, at }; for the OPEN (degraded)
  6105	    70	//     path the entry additionally carries degradedSeal: true (PIN: that flag lives
  6106	    71	//     on the receive_log entry). The sealed path's entry has NO truthy degradedSeal.
  6107	    72	//   - Returns { adopted: <truthy>, generation: <new gen>, archivedTo: <stem> }.
  6108	    73	//
  6109	    74	// STALENESS (prepare warnings, plan §Receive): OPEN source bundle -> a warning
  6110	    75	//   notes the missing seal; age > 12h (now - finalizedAt), HEAD moved
  6111	    76	//   (bundle.git.headSha !== opts.gitSnapshot.headSha), and a dirty mismatch each
  6112	    77	//   add a warning. Asserted as substring matches, not byte-exact strings.
  6113	    78	//
  6114	    79	// All txn tests seed: the bundle, baton.config.json at <root>, AND the built-in
  6115	    80	// signature table at its module-resolved path (loadSignatures reads via io.fs), so
  6116	    81	// role resolution and reasonClass classification both work over memfs.
  6117	    82	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  6118	    83	
  6119	    84	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  6120	    85	const repoRoot = join(here, '..', '..');
  6121	    86	// The exact path txn resolves for the built-in table (core/src/receive ->
  6122	    87	// ../../data), so seeding it here makes loadSignatures(io) succeed over memfs.
  6123	    88	const SIG_PATH = join(repoRoot, 'core', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
  6124	    89	const SIG_CONTENT = readFileSync(SIG_PATH, 'utf8');
  6125	    90	
  6126	    91	const ROOT = '/repo';

  6128	    93	
  6129	    94	const CONFIG = {
  6130	    95	  schema: 'baton/config@1',

  6143	   108	const CONFIG_TEXT = JSON.stringify(CONFIG, null, 2);

  6145	   110	// A git snapshot as core/src/git/snapshot.mjs would return; headSha matches the
  6146	   111	// bundle's captured git so the non-stale tests raise no HEAD-moved warning.
  6147	   112	const G1 = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'gd-1' };
  6148	   113	const G2 = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'gd-2-CHANGED' };
  6149	   114	
  6150	   115	const snapText = (b) => JSON.stringify(b, null, 2) + '\n';
  6151	   116	
  6152	   117	function baseBundle(overrides = {}) {
  6153	   118	  return {
  6154	   119	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
  6155	   120	    bundleId: 'b_txn0000000000',
  6156	   121	    generation: 1,
  6157	   122	    createdAt: NOW,
  6158	   123	    updatedAt: NOW,
  6159	   124	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 'sess-A', unstable: false },
  6160	   125	    task: { goal: 'Wire the receive transaction', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
  6161	   126	    plan: { steps: [{ id: 's1', title: 'Bind the token', status: 'active', note: null }] },
  6162	   127	    decisions: [],
  6163	   128	    files: { touched: [] },
  6164	   129	    roles: { assignments: {} },
  6165	   130	    git: { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'd0', summaryTruncated: false },
  6166	   131	    handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
  6167	   132	    journalSeq: 5,
  6168	   133	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
  6169	   134	    dedupeRing: [],
  6170	   135	    ...overrides,
  6171	   136	  };
  6172	   137	}
  6173	   138	
  6174	   139	function sealedBundle(overrides = {}) {
  6175	   140	  return baseBundle({
  6176	   141	    handoff: { status: 'sealed', reason: "You've hit your usage limit", reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: 'codex', finalizedAt: NOW, receive_log: [] },
  6177	   142	    ...overrides,
  6178	   143	  });
  6179	   144	}
  6180	   145	
  6181	   146	function openBundle(overrides = {}) {
  6182	   147	  return baseBundle({ handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] }, ...overrides });
  6183	   148	}
  6184	   149	
  6185	   150	function makeTxnIo({ bundle, journal, configText = CONFIG_TEXT, now = NOW, extra = {} } = {}) {
  6186	   151	  const files = {
  6187	   152	    [SIG_PATH]: SIG_CONTENT,
  6188	   153	    [`${ROOT}/baton.config.json`]: configText,
  6189	   154	    [`${ROOT}/.handoff/bundle.json`]: snapText(bundle),
  6190	   155	    ...extra,
  6191	   156	  };
  6192	   157	  if (journal !== undefined) files[`${ROOT}/.handoff/journal.ndjson`] = journal;
  6193	   158	  return makeIo({ files, now });
  6194	   159	}
  6195	   160	
  6196	   161	// Standard opts for a claude-code -> codex receive.
  6197	   162	function opts(overrides = {}) {
  6198	   163	  return { platform: 'codex', origin: 'claude-code', reason: 'switching to codex', gitSnapshot: G1, probes: null, sessionHint: 'codex-sess-1', ...overrides };
  6199	   164	}
  6200	   165	
  6201	   166	const readHistoryFreeze = (io, stem) => JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${bundlePaths(ROOT).historyDir}/${stem}.json`, 'utf8'));

  6204	   169	describe('receive-txn.prepare — sealed bundle', () => {

  6259	   224	describe('receive-txn.commit — sealed -> received (normal), ownership adoption + new generation', () => {
  6260	   225	  it('archives via rotateJournal "receive", adopts the receiver as owner, opens generation+1, appends receive_log', () => {
  6261	   226	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  6262	   227	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, opts(), io);
  6263	   228	
  6264	   229	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, opts(), io);
  6265	   230	
  6266	   231	    assert.ok(res.adopted, 'commit reports adoption');
  6267	   232	    assert.equal(res.generation, 2, 'a fresh writable generation is opened (1 -> 2)');
  6268	   233	    assert.ok(typeof res.archivedTo === 'string' && res.archivedTo.endsWith('.receive'), 'archivedTo is the receive rotation stem');
  6269	   234	
  6270	   235	    // A "receive" rotation landed in history.
  6271	   236	    const names = io.fs.readdirSync(bundlePaths(ROOT).historyDir);
  6272	   237	    assert.ok(names.some((n) => /\.receive\.json$/.test(n)), 'a receive freeze exists in history/');
  6273	   238	
  6274	   239	    // The live bundle: adopted owner, open, gen 2, receive_log appended.
  6275	   240	    const { bundle } = loadBundle(ROOT, io);
  6276	   241	    assert.equal(bundle.generation, 2);
  6277	   242	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.status, 'open', 'the new live generation is writable (open)');
  6278	   243	    assert.equal(bundle.origin.platform, 'codex', 'ownership adopted to the receiving platform');
  6279	   244	    assert.equal(bundle.origin.sessionHint, 'codex-sess-1', 'ownership adopted to the receiving session');
  6280	   245	    const entry = bundle.handoff.receive_log.at(-1);
  6281	   246	    assert.ok(entry, 'a receive_log entry was appended');
  6282	   247	    assert.equal(entry.origin, 'claude-code', 'receive_log records the intake origin');
  6283	   248	    assert.equal(entry.reason, 'switching to codex', 'receive_log records the intake reason');
  6284	   249	    assert.ok(typeof entry.at === 'string' && entry.at.length > 0, 'receive_log records a timestamp');
  6285	   250	    assert.ok(!entry.degradedSeal, 'a sealed->received commit is NOT a degraded seal');
  6286	   251	  });
  6287	   252	
  6288	   253	  it('(F7) the archived freeze is the RECEIVED seal with receipt data, not the pre-commit sealed bundle', () => {
  6289	   254	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });

  6291	   256	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, opts(), io);
  6292	   257

  6295	   260	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reason, "You've hit your usage limit", 'the finalize reason is retained on the archived seal');
  6296	   261	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reasonClass, 'usage-limit', 'the finalize reasonClass is retained');
  6297	   262	
  6298	   263	    const archived = seal.handoff.receive_log.at(-1);
  6299	   264	    assert.ok(archived, 'the archived seal retains the receive_log receipt data for this receive');
  6300	   265	    assert.equal(archived.origin, 'claude-code');
  6301	   266	    assert.equal(archived.reason, 'switching to codex');

  6307	   272	describe('receive-txn.commit — open -> received (degraded seal)', () => {

  6331	   296	
  6332	   297	  it('(F10) a LOW-confidence classification is NOT sealed — reasonClass stays null (no laundering)', () => {
  6333	   298	    // A cursor "quota exceeded" reason matches usage-limit only at LOW
  6334	   299	    // confidence. Sealing it would make the NEXT prepare trust it as high
  6335	   300	    // confidence and auto-avoid the origin. The degraded seal must leave
  6336	   301	    // reasonClass null so the next receive re-classifies at its true confidence.
  6337	   302	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: openBundle(), journal: '' });
  6338	   303	    const o = opts({ origin: 'cursor', reason: 'quota exceeded' });
  6339	   304	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, o, io);
  6340	   305	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, o, io);
  6341	   306	
  6342	   307	    const seal = readHistoryFreeze(io, res.archivedTo);
  6343	   308	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'a low-confidence class is not laundered into the seal');
  6344	   309	
  6345	   310	    // And the fresh open generation likewise carries no trusted class.
  6346	   311	    const { bundle } = loadBundle(ROOT, io);
  6347	   312	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'the new open generation inherits no low-confidence class');
  6348	   313	  });
  6349	   314	
  6350	   315	  it('(F10) an EXPLICIT --reason-class still seals (confirmed intake is high-confidence)', () => {
  6351	   316	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: openBundle(), journal: '' });
  6352	   317	    const o = opts({ origin: 'cursor', reason: 'quota exceeded', reasonClass: 'usage-limit' });
  6353	   318	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, o, io);
  6354	   319	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, o, io);
  6355	   320	    assert.equal(readHistoryFreeze(io, res.archivedTo).handoff.reasonClass, 'usage-limit', 'explicit intake seals');
  6356	   321	  });
  6357	   322	
  6358	   323	  it('(iter-4 I3) the fresh open generation carries NO prior reason/class/finalizedAt/toPlatformHint', () => {
  6359	   324	    // Even a HIGH-confidence sealed usage-limit receive must not leave its class
  6360	   325	    // on the new writable generation: the next prepare treats a sealed
  6361	   326	    // reasonClass as high-confidence and would auto-avoid the freshly adopted
  6362	   327	    // origin. Only receive_log (the audit chain) carries forward.
  6363	   328	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  6364	   329	    const o = opts();
  6365	   330	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, o, io);
  6366	   331	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, o, io);
  6367	   332	    assert.equal(res.generation, 2);
  6368	   333	
  6369	   334	    const { bundle } = loadBundle(ROOT, io);
  6370	   335	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.status, 'open', 'a fresh writable generation');
  6371	   336	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reason, null, 'no carried reason');
  6372	   337	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'no carried class (would launder as high-confidence next hop)');
  6373	   338	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.finalizedAt, null, 'no carried finalizedAt (would poison the staleness reference)');
  6374	   339	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.toPlatformHint, null, 'no carried toPlatformHint');
  6375	   340	    assert.ok(Array.isArray(bundle.handoff.receive_log) && bundle.handoff.receive_log.length >= 1, 'the receive_log audit chain is kept');
  6376	   341	  });
  6377	   342	
  6378	   343	  it('(iter-4 I3) a receive off the adopted generation does NOT auto-avoid the healthy origin', () => {
  6379	   344	    // End-to-end of the laundering fix: after a sealed usage-limit receive to
  6380	   345	    // claude-code, a NEXT receive (open generation, no seal) must re-classify at
  6381	   346	    // true confidence, not trust a carried class → claude-code stays selectable.

  6383	   348	    const o1 = opts(); // claude-code -> codex per the helper default
  6384	   349	    commit(ROOT, prepare(ROOT, o1, io).token, o1, io);

  6386	   351	    // (non-limit) reason; the carried class must not drive avoidance.
  6387	   352	    const o2 = opts({ platform: 'claude-code', origin: 'codex', reason: 'switching back', sessionHint: 'cc-2' });

  6389	   354	    const avoidedCodex = Object.values(assignments).every((/** @type {any} */ a) =>

  6391	   356	    );

  6393	   358	  });

  6985	     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
  6986	     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
  6987	     3	import { resolveRoles } from '../../core/src/roles/resolve.mjs';
  6988	     4	
  6989	     5	// ===========================================================================
  6990	     6	// Contract choices for roles/resolve.mjs. Source of truth: docs/design/core.md
  6991	     7	// §Module APIs (resolveRoles signature + assignment shape); plan §Core engine —
  6992	     8	// Role matrix + Availability probing (capability/outcome dimensions, avoid[],
  6993	     9	// native/delegated/forced-default/unavailable, chainIndex + skipped audit data).
  6994	    10	// resolveRoles is PURE and explainable.
  6995	    11	//
  6996	    12	// INPUT: resolveRoles({config, to, avoid = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null}).
  6997	    13	//   `config` is the PARSED config (roles as arrays of {platform, model, effort},
  6998	    14	//   as loadConfig returns) — built inline here so this file's red phase is
  6999	    15	//   attributable to resolve.mjs alone (not matrix.mjs).
  7000	    16	//
  7001	    17	// RETURN: { assignments: {[role]: Assignment}, notes: [] }.
  7002	    18	//   Assignment = {platform, model, effort, mode, chainIndex, skipped[], degraded?}.
  7003	    19	//   modes: 'native' | 'delegated' | 'forced-default' | 'unavailable'.
  7004	    20	//
  7005	    21	// PINS:
  7006	    22	//   1. CHAIN WALK. For each role, walk entries in order; the FIRST eligible
  7007	    23	//      (surviving) entry is selected. skipped[] records each passed-over entry
  7008	    24	//      as {chainIndex, platform, model, why} (extra fields tolerated).
  7009	    25	//      why values: 'avoided' | 'unknown-platform' | 'disabled' | 'rate-limited' | 'unauthenticated'.
  7010	    26	//   2. ELIGIBILITY / skip reasons:
  7011	    27	//        - platform ∈ avoid                          -> skip, why 'avoided'
  7012	    28	//        - platform NOT a key of config.platforms    -> skip, why 'unknown-platform'
  7013	    29	//        - config.platforms[p].enabled === false     -> skip, why 'disabled'
  7014	    30	//        - probes given & probes[p].outcome==='rate-limited' -> skip, why 'rate-limited'
  7015	    31	//        - probes given & capability==='installed' AND outcome!=='ok' (a VERIFIED
  7016	    32	//          auth/reachability FAILURE)                 -> skip, why 'unauthenticated'
  7017	    33	//          (iter-3 F1: a capped SUCCESS {installed, ok} leaves auth UNVERIFIED —
  7018	    34	//          it stays selectable and is flagged degraded, it is NOT skipped)
  7019	    35	//      A platform with capability ∈ {authenticated, reachable} AND outcome
  7020	    36	//      ≠ 'rate-limited' is eligible.
  7021	    37	//   3. DEGRADED. If probes is a non-null object but has NO entry for the chosen
  7022	    38	//      platform (unverifiable), the entry is selectable with degraded === true.
  7023	    39	//      probes === null means OFFLINE resolution: still selectable, but every
  7024	    40	//      selection is flagged degraded (gate-2 iter-2 M3; plan: offline always degraded).
  7025	    41	//      Non-degraded assignments have a falsy `degraded` (absent or false).
  7026	    42	//   4. MODE. First surviving entry: mode 'native' iff entry.platform === to,
  7027	    43	//      else 'delegated'. chainIndex = that entry's index in the ORIGINAL chain.
  7028	    44	//   5. nativeOnly restricts consideration to entries with platform === to; if
  7029	    45	//      none survive it MAY fall back to config.defaults[to] with mode
  7030	    46	//      'forced-default' (platform=to, model=defaults[to], effort=null,
  7031	    47	//      chainIndex=null) — but ONLY when the destination `to` is itself
  7032	    48	//      probe-eligible (PIN 2). When `to` is probe-INELIGIBLE (e.g. rate-limited),
  7033	    49	//      the forced-default is SUPPRESSED and the role resolves 'unavailable', the
  7034	    50	//      ineligibility audited in skipped[] (why 'rate-limited'). (fold C1-1)
  7035	    51	//   6. UNAVAILABLE. In normal (non-nativeOnly) mode, when NOTHING survives:
  7036	    52	//      mode 'unavailable', platform null, model null, effort null,
  7037	    53	//      chainIndex null, and skipped[] carries the full passed-over list.
  7038	    54	//   7. DETERMINISM. Same input -> deep-equal output; input config is not mutated.
  7039	    55	//      assignments object keys are in the CONFIG'S ROLE ORDER.
  7040	    56	//   8. avoid[] auto-population is the CALLER'S job; resolve only honors the list.
  7041	    57	// ===========================================================================
  7042	    58	
  7043	    59	const e = (platform, model, effort = null) => ({ platform, model, effort });
  7044	    60	
  7045	    61	const PLATFORMS = { 'claude-code': {}, codex: {}, cursor: {} };
  7046	    62	const DEFAULTS = { 'claude-code': 'claude-fable-5', codex: 'gpt-5.6-sol', cursor: 'composer' };
  7047	    63	
  7048	    64	// The full seven-role config in parsed form (mirrors the real baton.config.json).
  7049	    65	function fullConfig() {
  7050	    66	  return {
  7051	    67	    schema: 'baton/config@1',
  7052	    68	    roles: {
  7053	    69	      planner: [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5')],
  7054	    70	      'plan-reviewer': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5', 'xhigh')],
  7055	    71	      'test-author': [e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh')],
  7056	    72	      'test-verifier': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8')],
  7057	    73	      implementer: [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('cursor', 'composer')],
  7058	    74	      'final-reviewer-a': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh')],
  7059	    75	      'final-reviewer-b': [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8')],
  7060	    76	    },
  7061	    77	    platforms: { ...PLATFORMS },
  7062	    78	    defaults: { ...DEFAULTS },
  7063	    79	  };
  7064	    80	}
  7065	    81	
  7066	    82	// A small single-role config helper.
  7067	    83	function oneRole(chain) {
  7068	    84	  return { schema: 'baton/config@1', roles: { r: chain }, platforms: { ...PLATFORMS }, defaults: { ...DEFAULTS } };
  7069	    85	}
