The plan says `detect → finalize → remap → receive → relaunch` ([docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:66)), but `baton remap` is read-only ([core/src/commands/remap.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/remap.mjs:6)), and `receive` requires prepare/commit with identical bound inputs and session/root/probe/git state ([core/src/commands/receive.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/receive.mjs:33), [core/src/commands/receive.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/receive.mjs:64)). Probe cache and git state are token inputs ([core/src/receive/txn.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/receive/txn.mjs:65)), so any supervisor write, probe refresh, ff-sync, or smoke artifact change between prepare and commit can invalidate the token. — Fix: Replace the chain with an exact transaction: freeze child output, classify, finalize or degraded-open receive, refresh probes before prepare, prepare+commit with identical flags/session/root and no intervening writes, then relaunch. Add stale-token and retry tests around loop-state writes, git changes, probe-cache changes, and open-bundle limit death.
  7317	
  7318	4. [blocking] — “Role’s next chain entry” is not supported for model-level failures, including the degradation case in this review. The resolver skips by platform avoid/rate-limit/disabled/auth state, not by `{platform, model}` ([core/src/roles/resolve.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/roles/resolve.mjs:16)). It selects the first eligible entry in each configured role chain ([core/src/roles/resolve.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/roles/resolve.mjs:61)). If `codex/gpt-5.6-sol` is account-tier rejected but Codex itself is usable, the loop can retry the same rejected model forever or avoid all Codex entries and skip valid `codex/gpt-5.5` fallbacks. — Fix: Add model-entry failure state or an `avoidEntries[]` mechanism, classify model-unavailable separately from usage-limit, and test `gpt-5.6-sol rejected → codex/gpt-5.5 selected` without platform-wide avoidance.
  7319	
  7320	5. [major] — The worktree discipline lacks corruption guards. The plan says two persistent worktrees are gitignored and branch-jailed, with only the merger writing main ([docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:78)), but current `.gitignore` does not ignore `.worktrees/` ([.gitignore](/Users/saksham/baton/.gitignore:1)). It also does not define stale worktree adoption, branch-name namespace, dirty-worktree refusal, merge-conflict parking, concurrent main movement, or `git worktree remove/prune` cleanup. HYPOTHESIS: raw deletion of `.worktrees/` can leave Git’s linked-worktree metadata stale. — Fix: Define `worktrees.mjs` as a strict Git transaction layer: namespaced branches, clean-index preflight, expected branch/HEAD assertions before and after each child, main merge lock, ff-only sync checks, conflict parking, and prune/remove cleanup. Add adversarial tests for stale branches, dirty workers, main moved, merge conflict, and untracked `.worktrees/`.
  7321	
  7322	6. [major] — The attribution invariant is not actually covered for child worktree commits. The plan claims the existing doctor guard covers main and both worktree branches ([docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:89)), but current doctor scans only the last 50 commits reachable from the current repo invocation ([core/src/commands/doctor.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/doctor.mjs:229)). The hard repo invariant requires sole author and zero AI attribution everywhere ([AGENTS.md](/Users/saksham/baton/AGENTS.md:55)). — Fix: Make the supervisor set `GIT_AUTHOR_*` and `GIT_COMMITTER_*` for every child, disable Claude attribution settings where applicable, and gate every branch merge on `git log` scans over the exact worker branch and main range for author/email plus forbidden trailers. Acceptance must include child commits from both worktrees.
  7323	
  7324	7. [major] — The supervisor spawn and verdict-tail contract is too weak for a v1 automation surface. The plan relies on plain final-tail parsing ([docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:52)), but does not specify process-group lifecycle, timeout kill/reap behavior, output truncation, prompt-echo defense, hook/log noise separation, or what happens when a child exits after edits but before a verdict. The existing detector classifies generic nonzero as `other-error`, not usage-limit ([core/src/detect/classifier.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/detect/classifier.mjs:81)). — Fix: Define child supervision as a contract: detached supervisor PID/lock, process groups, SIGTERM/SIGKILL escalation, transcript files with byte caps, parser over the child’s final assistant message only or structured output schema, and tests for prompt echo, multiple verdict tails, truncated transcript, timeout, zombie grandchild, and non-limit failure.
  7325	
  7326	8. [major] — The smoke gate can approve stale state. The plan parks after smoke and resumes with `--approve-smoke` ([docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:61)), but it does not bind approval to smoke output, git state, loop state, or the exact command. Existing git snapshots include a content-sensitive digest that could be used for this ([core/src/git/snapshot.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/git/snapshot.mjs:17)). — Fix: Write a smoke approval record with digest of loop state, command, output, HEAD, dirty/contentDigest, and child assignment. Require `--approve-smoke <token>` and revalidate before continuing; otherwise park for a fresh smoke run.

FINDINGS:
1. [blocking] — Root/session disposition is still defective for Layer-2 main-root children. The plan says the supervisor is the sole main-bundle writer and will pass `--root <mainRoot>` plus `--session loop-<runId>` so the foreign-session guard never fires ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:71), [plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:73)), but `baton checkpoint` does not accept `--session` today ([checkpoint.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs:163)), and unknown strict flags are rejected ([shared.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/shared.mjs:103)). Layer-2 children run in the main root ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:77)); existing root discovery will resolve that root without `--root` ([shared.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/shared.mjs:35)), and checkpoint rejects stable foreign sessions in an owned bundle ([checkpoint.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs:265)). Fix: specify and test the actual child-hook policy: either add a supported checkpoint session/owner flag and make child hooks use it, or disable/redirect child harness hooks for supervised children. Add a Layer-2 main-root concurrent-child checkpoint test, not only the two-worktree test in the plan ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:81)).
2. [blocking] — The smoke phase violates the repo TDD contract. AGENTS requires no implementation before failing tests exist and the test-verifier has approved them ([AGENTS.md](/Users/saksham/baton/AGENTS.md:27)); the revised plan still orders `smoke` before subtask TDD ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:38)), and says the implementer builds the smoke slice immediately after Gate 1 ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:121)). Acceptance constraint 1 encodes that same order: smoke first, then two TDD subtasks ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:224)). Fix: make the smoke slice itself a TDD mini-cycle: test-author writes the failing smoke assertion, test-verifier approves it, then implementer writes only enough code to pass it; update acceptance to assert that order.
3. [major] — Role-ID validation remains ambiguous. The plan says loop role references are by ID only and unknown roles fail validation ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:46)), and it adds `subtask-reviewer` ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:51)), but the default phase list still names `implement` and `subtask-review` rather than the matrix role IDs `implementer` and `subtask-reviewer` ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:38), [baton.config.json](/Users/saksham/baton/baton.config.json:8)). Fix: define phase IDs separately from role IDs in the `loop.json` schema, or use only matrix role IDs in the default phase spec.
4. [major] — The failover drift acceptance is partly unfalsifiable as written. The plan says stale-token tests inject drift between receive prepare and commit via loop-state writes, git changes, and probe refreshes ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:143)), but the existing receive token binds bundle/config/git/probe/platform/session inputs, not `.handoff/loop/state.json` itself ([txn.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/receive/txn.mjs:57), [txn.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/receive/txn.mjs:80)). `.handoff/` is ignored ([.gitignore](/Users/saksham/baton/.gitignore:2)), and git snapshots use `--exclude-standard` for untracked files ([snapshot.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/git/snapshot.mjs:30)). Fix: either require loop-position writes to mutate the bound handoff bundle before prepare/after commit, or replace the loop-state drift expectation with a loop transaction lock/no-write assertion.
5. [major] — The locked “survives operator session ending” requirement is still untestable. The plan states the supervisor CLI must survive the operator session ending ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:25)), but the child contract only detaches child process groups ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:99)), and the acceptance suite does not include supervisor SIGHUP/parent-death/restart behavior ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:224)). Fix: define foreground vs detached supervisor semantics, add a supervisor run lock/recovery story, and add an acceptance test that kills the launching session and verifies continue-or-park recovery.

The plan states the supervisor CLI must survive the operator session ending ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:25)), but the child contract only detaches child process groups ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:99)), and the acceptance suite does not include supervisor SIGHUP/parent-death/restart behavior ([plan](/Users/saksham/baton/docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md:224)). Fix: define foreground vs detached supervisor semantics, add a supervisor run lock/recovery story, and add an acceptance test that kills the launching session and verifies continue-or-park recovery.
